Like A Dagger or a Shoe
12/30/2024, 9:12:18 PM
Runners
Otters aka Roadkill
Heat
Aradia
Echo
Ruxpin

As the team is still processing the distressing news about the capabilities of the Discidium weapon, and The Collector and Kangee Ohanzee are debating next steps to try to keep Prot0 from using it, Ruxpin interrupts to inquire with them about what’s going on with all his missing team-mates returning as Chimera. This gets their attention (briefly) diverted, and they share some intel, and promise to keep an eye out for more of his team popping up, and Ruxpin says he’ll do the same, with both sides agreeing to share intel if/when. With that, TC and KO both take their leave to deal with this new issue, and Arcane also says his goodbyes for the time being.

Otters drives the van down remotely to pick them up again, while they talk through some of what they just experienced on the metaplanes. They also bemoan StepMAMA’s absence, and promote the completely unemotional, all biz Paris (Echo’s Agent program) to her former role keeping an eye on the TacNet and their WAN. Perri updates them with some of her write-ups of the UnITy data they helped develop and prove, and puts Heat’s name in the research credit area as requested. She says that Celedyr has gotten onboard with the theory and will be promoting it alongside her launching it on various shadow forums. They also hear about some kind of massive AZT corporate breach- apparently McClure delivered on his promise of some backdoors and access, but AZT has been coming down hard on shadow forums, mods, hackers and the like since.

Perri also tells them that the Nexus has been funding runs to try to backtrace the Null to their homeworld after their recent attack on the Nexus, but that these runs have proven to be very dangerous and often lethal for TM’s and AI/EI’s, so they’ve turned to mostly using standard deckers, who the Null seem to view more like odd curiosities that major threats, if they don’t aggro the Null. Aradia gets the word out through Dean Costello of high-paying, high-risk missions for interested deckers, to help recruit additional help. Perri says she and Arcane have also been wanting to try to figure out how to get to Dis as well as wherever the Null come from, but that this will likely take longer to set up, but that they hope the team will be interested in the job when it’s ready.

Heat finally gets to do a write-up to get on Jackpoint, with T3chK1d (who seems a little world-wearier than his youthful appearance would suggest) providing the necessary reference. Heat focuses on the team’s time in Caelum (leaving out the weapon details and how exactly they arrived), alongside their transit to The Grey Lands and details on that place, partially to warn anyone else who finds themselves in Caelum. He gets his provisional membership, and a lot of interested inquiries, though he’s careful not to double sell any intel or mention Vhortas. They all load in the van once it finally arrives, and head back the safehouse to rest.

The next morning, they get a message from Stiletto, requesting a meet in the Aurora Warrens. This one seems NOT to be a trap, so they go, and meet her near to the Paradise Lost mall/zone. She tells them she got some critical intel that The Auditor is hosting a meeting with some corpo head honchos, involving some kind of tech demo. She knows the location and time, but not who will be there or what will be discussed or demonstrated, and wants to find out. So she wants to hire the team to get her in and help surveil the thing. They agree, and gear up at the mall shops nearby to get ready.

Otters flies Morticia down to get an initial overview, and sees that Stiletto’s intel looks good- there’s definitely some activity on the long-abandoned USAF academy campus, with a lot of movement centered on one of the more intact buildings. There are 4 sniper teams with good LOS covering the interior of the campus as well as all the obvious approaches, and a mini-drone aerial perimeter. The ground has also been made more hostile by releasing a large amount of Ethereal Centipedes, which as part of their toxins force their victims to Assense, making them very magically visible. The centipedes are kept from the busy area by some chemicals circling the structure.

Without being able to get a good visual inside the main structure, they plan to infiltrate on foot as well. Ruxpin and Echo will take out one sniper team and pose as them, with Heat looping or faking their comms and sensors as needed. Then, Heat, Aradia, and Stiletto will infiltrate the main building, with support from Otters via micro-drone, and attempt to figure out what’s going on. Aradia spends some time magically and physically disguising Ruxpin and Echo to match the sniper team they’ll be replacing, and Ruxpin sneaks in on foot with a masked invisibility spell on, while Echo gets dropped down via VTOL by Otters.

Echo blasts the spotter, dropping them in a single shot, as Ruxpin punches the sniper. Unfortunately, he is in human form and his blow doesn’t hit as hard as hoped, but he manages to get a shot off and finish off the sniper before he can call for aid. Echo quickly data-taps the spotter, with Heat diving in to swap in Echo and Ruxpin’s bio-monitor signals and monitor for any suspicion. Ruxpin and Echo hide the bodies and take up the positions, keeping an eye on things. They get a better view of the main building as well, and spot some guard patrols in the upper levels, helping Otters time VTOL drop-offs to get the other team in place without getting spotted.

That team sneaks down a few flights with the sniper team spotting for them, and Heat masking all their comms traffic. Heat uses his newest SkillSoft to pick a newly upgraded lock with Aradia’s aid, and they loose the drones into the main level. Otters spots some good hiding spots for the 3 inside team members in a nearby loading bay, so they stage there while the drones advance. They watch the feed, horrified to see a large collection of the stasis pods that they’ve seen before, almost all full and labelled as to the “quality” of the contents- mages, TM’s, SURGEd individuals, ghouls, vamps, etc- a wide sampling of different types of person, mostly with some special ability or other, though not all.

in an adjacent room, some techs are setting up some devices and running hardwire cables to and fro. No sign of the corpos or the Auditor yet, but Otters almost loses signal when a jammer is switched on. She tries to drive closer to get better feed, but almost gets spotted by snipers and abandons the approach, focusing on what additional noise reduction she can do. The inside team see the Auditor lead the group of a dozen corpos out, all of whom are wearing digitizing disguise masks, though Heat is focusing on their gait, clothes etc- other tells that can give them away in future once analyzed. The Auditor and the lead tech talk the corpos through their wide selection of subjects, telling them “the devices are almost ready, and they expect a near 93% success rate” whatever ominous news that means.

However, the Auditor interrupts the proceedings to say that AZT hasn’t held up their end of the bargain in terms of their security of information, and that an example must be made. After a subtle gesture, guards seize 3 of the corpos, one of who the team recognizes by his pretentious magic robe as Cehaulli, their AZT contact from Detroit. The three seized AZT reps, screaming their protests, are forced into stasis tubes and forcibly sedated, leaving them with horrifying rictus expression as the lids are lowered back over. With that done, the Auditor turns back the crowd, but this seems to have been enough for Stiletto, as she starts conjuring a fireball in her hands before Heat can stop her.

The Auditor looks up in their direction, seemingly detecting magic, and Otters and the snipers decide it’s distraction time. Otters watches on the drone feed as the Auditor calls for a fresh security sweep, and then promptly fries the drones with some kind of massive spell. Otters also suppressive fires one of the sniper nests, while Ruxpin starts picking off some of the others, with Echo on the comms posing as the spotter trying to sow chaos by claiming the other sniper’s that they are shooting at are imposters. This works for a moment, allowing Otters to VTOL two of the inside team back out, but then the other guards start verifying with code-phrases that Echo doesn’t know.

At this point they decide to bail, as Otters races the van around to grab them and flies the VTOL back in for one last pickup. Ruxpin and Echo replace the dead sniper team, hoping that will add a little more chaos, and dive into the van, as Otters cuts through the overgrown campus. They watch via Morticia’s overhead feed as a bunch of horrifying stitched-together Chimera are unleashed on the campus from some underground holding area, and start spreading. Picking up the 2 inside team members, they race away, with Morticia and the VTOL overhead, carrying the last member out- Heat, who insisted that “Creep” get out first, when he meant Aradia.

They make good their escape, and debrief with Stiletto, passing over the surveillance files. She apologizes for almost blowing it, but seemed to really want to take out some of those people when she could. She agrees getting the data out was the better move. She also confirms that the AZT data breach revealed some intel about their dealings with Dis, implying there’s an internal corp struggle going on. They assume that what they saw was a response or part of that then, but wonder what these devices are, and why they seem to run on metahuman batteries. Stiletto takes her leave and splits, saying she’ll be in touch when she has more for them, but not before arranging to ship a new prototype Grey Cell weapon to Ruxpin- a power first that can enhance his punches.

The team heads back to the safehouse, where Aradia gets a worried call from Kobold, who says she got a weird emergency message from Simonetta Chandra, and passes it along. It’s a video feed of Chandra racing down some kind of corp facility hallway while yelling at Aradia asking her “what has she brought them”, while occasional glimpses over her shoulder show what they thought was a child, the lone survivor or the missing AZT town, punching through CorpSec guards and gaining on Chandra until the feed cuts off in a splash of viscera. The kid definitely looks like the one they passed along to her, and Kobold confirms that the message looks to have been recorded around the same time the Auditor seized the other reps- so it looks like one faction may be cleaning out the other within AZT. Aradia calls Juan Xiuhuitl in Chicago to warn him to keep his head down, but loyal corp soldier that he is, he says if there are purges he is sure they are necessary, though he seems to appreciate the warning, and offers Aradia more work should she ever find herself back in the Windy City.

They rest, fitfully, and awaken to a visibly distraught and perhaps drunk, definitely crying Le Masque, who says that he wishes to hire them for some revenge. After some inquiries, the team uncovers that he appears to have been Cehualli’s lover, and somehow got news of his demise, and now wants to go after the best target he has for those responsible- a factory MCT set up to make more of Vishala’s horrifying Spirit/Sprite torture weapons. He has the location and says he’ll give the team the day to scout it out and make a plan before it’s time to go in. Heat isn’t happy with his negotiation on this one, but they all agree, and agree that these dangerous weapons must be stopped. Echo orders some heavier armor in preparation, and the rest of them check their weapons and ammo and get ready to go surveilling.

Place of Clouds to Zecorporatum to Denver
12/23/2024, 12:39:48 PM
Runners
Otters aka Roadkill
Heat
Aradia
Echo
Ruxpin

The team heads to Wuxing’s facility, and Aradia is delighted to encounter her old friend from the Battle of Chicago, Lu Wendao. He takes them to a private meeting room, where they make a deal to receive lodging, food and use of Wuxing’s portal to send the data packet they received from Aubrey Carter to Ellis, along with a short explanation Aradia writes up. Wendao tells them that Wuxing isn’t yet able to transit large quantities of people and materiel, but is very interested in learning from the Great Portal to expand their abilities in that realm. Echo tries to plant the idea that having a big, open, stable portal anywhere on Earth is a deadly invasion risk, but Wendao is not yet dissuaded.

In exchange, the team hands over one of the samples of strange rock that Heat and Otters collected from the ziggurat, as well as their mapping data and intel from Vhortas, with no info about how they came to be there. Wendao is delighted at the trade, and hosts them to a lovely Cantonese- style dinner with some astral ingredient substitutes. They sleep well, and the next day head to Horizon’s hotel/office, a combo of local and imported materials made to impress. After baldly describing their purpose to an overly friendly receptionist, they are quickly ushered back to the office of one Mx. Maud, seemingly another Dawkins Group operative.

They seem to know of Heat and Hype’s relationship, and perhaps viewing Heat as an ally of a rival is fairly dismissive of him. However, Echo and Aradia smooth things over and the team makes a deal to get access to a customized airship through Horizon to use for their job, in exchange for doing a celebrity escort mission to Horizon Resort Island on the way. Otters excitedly presents her plans to Mx. Maud, who says they will need about a day to mod one of their ships to suit. The team kills time around the Spire, with many watching the airship modification at the docks, a fascinating process. Their new ship, freshly christened the “No Strings Attached”, seems to be of the classic livewood airship stlye, rather than some of the manta ray/turtle, or other forms they see.

Hopping aboard once it’s ready, Otters straps into the “captain’s coffin”, the closest the dock carpenters could get to a rigger cocoon on short notice, and gets a feel for the rigging. It seems to operate on a combination of the principles of air and sea travel, which she it luckily well versed in. Heat loads up the barbed harpoon in the ballista, and they take aboard their VIP- a celebrity runner named Starstike, whose popular streaming channel Otters is mildly familiar with. Heat knows that Horizon is trying to recruit SS and others like him, and assumes this is kind of a co-production PR event to try to get him to sign on.

They all quickly find him pretty annoying, and Ruxpin ducks below deck and swaps back to human form, with SS disappointed that the bear he saw seems to have been dock crew and not ship crew. Echo gets some masks and costumes for the team at the Horizon tourist shop, along with a captain’s tricorn and epaulets for Otters to wear in the coffin. SS has a support crew along with- a rigger running cam drones, who the team tries to avoid, though SS assures them dismissively that any identifying details will be edited out. There is also a woman running hair and makeup, doing quick touchups between shots, a lawyer/manager type in a slick suit, and a subtle bodyguard who smoothly blends into the background and also avoids the camdrones.

The manager tries to get the team to sign some stuff, but when they refuse until it it reviewed by their lawyer he notarizes it anyway and says asking was a formality since they are aboard corporate property of their own free will. They get under way, not especially happy about who they’re carrying. Ruxpin mostly stays below and out of sight, and the trip goes smoothly. They arrive at Horizon Resort Island to find that the promo images at the hotel are more aspirational than realistic in terms of the current state of construction. SS seems delighted at the opportunity to rough it for a while, while most of the rest of his crew seems distressed at the unexpectedly non-indoors condition of much of the visible space. That said, it’s a beautiful island, and SS eagerly strikes out, avoiding the marked paths and head straight into dense jungle to make his way overland to the reverse waterfall he’s heard tell of.

With the rest of his crew following somewhat morosely, the team shouts that they’ll be back in a day or two for pickup, and heads out. They begin flying a perimeter while keeping an eye out, looking for the style of mining vessel they were told to expect, hoping to spot and intercept them before they get into the protected space near to the Spire. They do eventually see the ship after a while searching, it seems to be skirting the edge of the Great Storm. As the approach, with Aradia magically camouflaging their vessel to appear like an unarmed trading ship, they see that the crew of 7 looks a bit stressed and seems to be trying to hurry the ship along with some magical wind.

As they begin to approach, hoping their cover will hold long enough to start a conversation, they see one of the Dis Seeker ships emerge from the storm, seemingly ignoring the heavy winds and moving solely under magical power. One of the massive dino/drake Chimera leaps aboard the former MCT mining ship and tears the mage filling the sails in two, tossing the chunks overboard. Another Chimera, this one looking like some sort of human/bird hybrid, also flaps over, and bites one of the toughs on the mining ship, inflicting some kind of deadly poison. As Ruxpin scopes in on the targets, and Otters races the ship forward to get in range, Ruxpin sees that this second Chimera is another missing member of his squad!!

He takes the shot after Heat tells him to, and badly injures his altered former friend. The Dis ship takes a shot with some kind of keel-mounted beam weapon that Otters barely avoids, and Otters returns fire with the cannon, missing the Dis ship and badly damaging the miner’s vessel. She also manages to damage the larger chimera in another explosion, though the mining vessel is further damaged as well. Heat gets the barbed bolt lodged into the mining vessel as well, and some rat/child Chimera leap over from the Dis ship and start racing up the rope towards the NSA.

As the defenders on the mining ship are obviously out-matched, Ruxpin snipes the larger chimera, damaging it further, but also sees his old squad-mate poison another tough, as the big Chimera tosses another overboard. The 2nd mage on the ship also shrivels and dies- there is likely at least one mage on the Disian vessel. Heat, desperate to get something from this encounter, touchlinks to the mining vessel through the metal lined harpoon cable, and tells the rigger to flee to the NSA for safety and giver over control of their ship. The rigger briefly tries to negotiate before seeing how badly the tides have turned and calling the abandon ship.

He starts fleeing up the harpoon line as well, with Echo blasting the two rat/child chimera off with his shotgun. Otters fails to dodge the next shot from the Disians and takes some massive damage, with a hold clean through the hull. Aradia provides some leadership to all involved, and levitates the rigger to help, while the rigger leaves most of his drones behind to cover his escape. Unfortunately, the last tough is also left behind, unable to break free. Otters finally gets a hit on the Disian ship, but the explosion destroys the miner’s vessel, which starts coming apart in the sky and slowly dropping bits and cargo. Ruxpin yanks the harpoon free as the rigger hangs on desperately, assisted by levitation, and Heat and Ruxpin start hauling him in.

Seeing that the Disian ship is retreating into the clouds of the Great Storm again, and seemingly magically pulling the spilled cargo towards them, Otters decides to run for it, and books it, using some nearby islands as cover as Aradia magically changes the ship’s appearance again. The rigger, Artur Carolina, urges them to set down and helps Otters do some repairs on the badly damaged vessel, getting it functionally restored, though still without armor. Meanwhile, the rest of the team pulls a sneaky con, with Heat editing the footage of the battle to insert Starstrike into all the heroic moments, and putting together a data packet to send to Mx. Maud to hopefully get the team off the hook for damage to the ship. Echo and Aradia focus on tailoring the message to MM based on their interactions and observations, and they feel like they have a pretty good package by the time the ship is patched.

They head back to Horizon Island, and pick up the VIP and crew. SS seems delighted by his adventures and sight-seeing, while the crew looks half-feral from their day and night in the jungle, and very relieved to see the ship return, aside from the bodyguard, who looks smooth and unblemished and slips aboard without comment. Otters does some fine flying, giving the cam drones something to look at, while SS poses in front of various stunning vistas. Meanwhile, Ruxpin and Artur stay below and out of sight, with Artur using his last few micro-drones to keep an eye above-decks.

As they approach the Spire, they see a damaged Dis Seeker docked, which looks like it could be the one they encountered. Artur suggests they send their conned data from here, pass pilot controls to SS’ rigger, and get out of here via his backup plan exit. The team agrees, and Aradia gets an offer to work as a producer for Horizon based on how well she sells the reel they send. The surprised cam drone rigger takes control of the ship as the team all head belowdecks, with SS bidding them farewell and seemingly thrilled to see where his tour of the metaplanes takes him next. Artur pulls out a strange Tarot card- it seems like one of the alt decks version of the World, aka The Awakened World in the 6WT.

Having seen this used before, the team is able to gently help Artur activate it correctly without revealing how much they know about Tarot, and they choose to make a door back to Prime Software in Zecorp, wanting to finish their biz there and see if Heat’s moto is intact. They step through and find themselves back in the lab there, amidst some very surprised scientists and guards. They insist on a meeting with Salazar, who quickly arrives. They get the feeling he may have been planning to ambush them on their return but gave up when they were gone much longer than expected- it seems they wouldn’t be the first team lost to Caelum.

They pass over the shittier digital weapons that Scriniarii gave them, which Salazar seems a little non-plussed by, but he signals to the guards to let them go, since without an ambush this would be too messy. They head outside and Heat finds his bike both stripped of parts and fully shifted to the local paradigm. Not wanting to deal with it further and a little sad, he tosses the key fob to a surprised Salazar, who delegates some of his minions to haul it inside, seemingly a little disgusted. The team heads back to Barkins’ office, and finds Kenda de Santos awaiting them there. She takes possession of Artur, promising him safety from corp/Dis retribution in exchange for his info. She also tells the team they have earned more favor with Grey Cell, and they will have further work soon.

They exit, and the team is surprised as Prot0 enters. He asks for his copy of the Discidium, which after some suspicion and back and forth, they agree to provide so long as stays with them until they meet with The Collector and pass his copy along. Prot0 and Heat tagteam decrypting it, which turns out to be good as it was quite difficult. Prot0 gets his copy and seems content, but in a hurry to return to the material plane. Heat first pays Barkins to stay on retainer for future lawyerly needs, and they all turn over the documents that Starstike’s lawyer wanted them to sign, which seem like common indemnity waivers, and Barkins tells them not to worry.

They head out into the street, where Prot0 hops into his stealthed spider tank drone, and they get Arcane’s plant cat to blip over and get him to open the portal. He does so, and they exit into some abandoned building in the southern/rural FRFZ. Prot0 quickly takes off as they get their bearings, finding Arcane there along with The Collector, and a colleague he introduces as Kangee Ohanzee, who is delighted to see that his contact Ruxpin is working out well with the team. Ruxpin makes introductions and vouches for Ohanzee, with TC eagerly examining the Discidium. He reports back and confirms some of Heat’s worries- while this is a matrix weapon that could likely effect the Null, it might be too powerful, and could possibly destroy Foundation Hosts or sever them from the Foundation, and the effect might not be contained and could even be contagious.

Given that, it requires further study and may be too dangerous to use, with the risk of collateral damage. The team interjects here, telling TC that they also gave a copy to Prot0, which he isn’t happy about, but he says it’s done and they’ll have to deal with it. He pays up, and congratulates them on a strange and hard mission, as he heads out to do some crisis management. Ruxpin catches him and Ohanzee before they all take off though, as he wants to discuss what it means that his team seems to have been turned into Chimera….

Caelum to the Grey Lands to Vhortas to the Place of Clouds
12/16/2024, 2:12:03 PM
Runners
Otters aka Roadkill
Heat
Aradia
Echo
Ruxpin

With the Discidium (as well as the lesser distraction weapons for Prime Software) in hand, the team prepares to head out for Zecorporatum. Scriniarii seems interested in their offer of ongoing information exchanges, and StepMAMA announces that she is going to remain behind, embodied, for a while, in order to continue learning and researching, as well as experimenting with having a physical body. After some thorough questioning by Heat and Echo confirms that she seems to be making the choice of her own free will and isn’t being delusional about potential risks, they say their goodbyes for now, with her promising to return before long, as she has both the team and her other matrix friends and allies to consider.

The team leaves her behind once Echo retrieves Paris, finding them a little more powerful of an Agent than before, but not having experienced an Emergence. StepMAMA goes back over the Spark/Ei/etc as a refresher, and the team returns to Legatum to get “teleported” back to Zecorp. They strap into the chairs, and as the machine fires up and they are overwhelmed by the lights flashing on them, they hear Legatum apologizing, but saying that it cannot allow this weapon to be distributed. Apparently all of the disapproval they encountered in their search for it has some consequences after all! As they flash back into some other reality, they find themselves in a shoddier, crumbling dome, which seems to have further cracked and fallen apart with the shock of their arrival.

Unstrapping, they do a quick recce. Aradia finds that this world is magically dead, again, but more like a mana void than like Caelum’s strange absence of all biologicals. She checks and confirms with Echo and Ruxpin that their magic is also offline still. Heat manages to spot some distant electronic communications, and feels like he gains access to some TM abilities as he begins to almost understand them- they seem to share some familiarity with Earth tech, but have evolved along some kind of branching path from a common ancestor. Otters is unable to connect to it, so once again the Noosphere seems proven. They don’t seem to be in Zecorp, for sure, and Ruxpin spots the glow of lights of two different cities, one a little larger than the other.

Aradia also finds some expended canisters of some sort hardwired into the dome’s exterior. A bunch of the circuits and tech look jury-rigged and now thoroughly fried, and the canisters all look empty. Confirming with the team that these seem not dissimilar in function/intent of use to the manavoltaic rods they encountered in Denver, they assume that one or more of these rods provided the magical charge to the device that allowed them to travel here from the non-magical land of Caelum, but that either just through use or possibly because of the mana void, this has permanently damaged the machine and drained all the energy, so even if they knew how to work it, they have no way to power it to try to get home (or at least to Zecorp).

As they walk, Aradia and Ruxpin spot some dug up areas, and with some further investigation, they identify these as open pit mines that seem to have extracted Fovaeum, aka Void Rock. This is the strange anti-magic substance that they first encountered on the Mile High Realm, as a threat delivered by Disian agents to local law enforcement, such as it is. Apparently this is the (or one of the) places it comes from, implying there is at least some way to get to and from here occasionally, and hopefully in addition to the path via which they arrived. As they continue, they see that the mining seems to be clustered around grid points, as if the Void Rock has some kind of oddly even distribution, which could explain the dangerous mana void vibes in the astral.

The whole place seems grey, washed out, and despondent. The team all notices their ShadowCloaks getting a little more ragged, and Echo’s is gone entirely- uh-oh! The drones are also looking worse than ever- flesh embedded with smooth metal and plastic bits, turning a sickly grey. They embark towards the smaller city, with Echo deciding they’ll be likelier to be able to find useful intel/access to good contacts there. Heat starts to decipher some comms, and figures out that the 2 cities seem to be on the brink of a military engagement that neither side wants but also neither will back down from. Seeing a refugee column fleeing the rear of the city towards some nearby hills, they redirect towards that, not wanting to get caught in shelling in an unfamiliar city.

They also see an impressive array of surveillance and artillery devices, though once the tech seems kinda off- as if the material plane had split off around the time of the Awakening and developed further tech but without magic, nano, etc, in a state of constant war. Reaching the column, they find the expected civilians, but also patrols of camouflaged soldiers splitting off to set up pickets guarding the backline, and Commissars overseeing the whole effort, who they are quickly warned to avoid after talking to some of the people here. Oddly, everyone appears to be human- no metatypes, no obvious spirits, and Aradia is fairly sure spirits wouldn’t be able to survive here.

Ruxpin reluctantly allows Heat to ride him, with Otters already riding the Mule, to try to somewhat conceal the oddness of their party by posing as traveling entertainers caught up in the war against their will. Some of the old-timers and children are friendly enough but they get a lot of suspicious looks, but they do hear rumors that the caves they are headed to for shelter have some unsafe and mysterious deeper levels, which sounds promising as a potential planar transition points. Their only other lead is trying to find a crossing in the clouds to the Place of Clouds, but even with the looming grey sky weighing heavily on them, they can’t find any vertical elements of the terrain within sight to get them high enough. So they head underground.

In the rush for beds, chambers etc in the crudely carved hillside shelter system, they slip past the refugees and Commissars, and head deeper into the caves. Hearing some cries of dismay from behind them, Heat and Aradia decide to turn back to try to see what happened, but are quickly spotted, denounced as spies, and forced to flee back into the deeper tunnels, where it seems the people of this realm are unwilling to go. Aradia does feel some distant, sporadic shaking though, so it seems the shelling may have begun. Using Ruxpin’s nose and Heat’s knowledge of mazes, hard-learned at the Seelie Court, they navigate deeper, with Aradia finding that the magical suppression becomes less oppressive as they go.

Unfortunately as they navigate deeper in the pitch black cave system, Aradia’s nose ring starts to glow- bugs are about! Checking the walls more carefully, they find that they have gone from human-expanded cave, through natural cave, to bug-dug cave. Lacking other options, and feeling their magic return, even though it has a sickening twist to it, they continue on. This is especially necessary as Echo was starting to be effected by the despondency of the Grey Lands without their Cloak to protect them. The team is also getting dangerously low on supplies and needs to rest soon so they push on. They emerge into a large grotto, surrounded with tunnels inhabited by different species of bugs, their styles of nests and hives visible, all just barely expanding into the grotto but not filling it, oddly, given that bugs generally tend to swarm and expand as much as possible.

The magical members of the team hear some disturbing Whispers in their heads calling them to the tunnels, but it seems like all the different species overlapping creates enough cacophony that they are able to fend them off for now, but they are worried. Otters sends the fly spies ahead to scout, wanting to check out the odd structure in the center of the grotto- a massive tumbled stone temple, floating in a lake of ichor. The whole place has a sickly yellow/green tint, and the smell is appalling, a constant mix of death/rot/birth that nearly overwhelms Ruxpin, with his sensitive nose.

Otters spots signs of an ancient battle, using weaponry that looks like a historical version of the armaments they had seen aboveground, also back from around the time of the Awakening. There are also long rotted and desiccated insect corpses, which the team is surprised to see haven’t been consumed by the Hive. The team starts to speculate that perhaps the people aboveground were subject to an bug invasion (Invae-sion?) early in their magical cycle, and used such terrible weaponry in repelling it that they killed their world’s mana before it could get a firm hold, explaining why it seems to be so bleak and dying there. Whatever was used here could also explain why the bugs haven’t reused the space. Regardless, the temple seems enterable through some cracks in it’s upper tier, though it is upsettingly non-Euclidean- it has square corners, but the drones can only capture 3 faces of it on feeds.

Aradia levitates them team over in two groups, given the bad background count and not wanting to risk contacting the ichor, especially as Otters’ drones have reported both radiological spikes and a combo of poisonous and unknown chemicals and gasses emanating from various of the tunnels off the chamber. They enter, and find what seems to be a well-kept stash and resupply spot. Otters ID’s the gear as ARES spec, probably for an armor suit weapon system or an ALPHA, so not stuff they can use (including the unfamiliar MRE’s), but there’s a large padded bedroll, and whatever stone this temple is made of seems to block the Whispers to some degree, so they feel safe resting here. Aradia does psychometry on the bedroll to try to figure out who they may be dealing with and when they may return, while Echo uses their adept power to try to decipher the strange glyphs covering the interior walls.

Heat wants to explore the rest of the temple, but with Ruxpin unable to fit in bear form and Otters unwilling, gives up on it for now. Aradia snaps out of her magic trance and reports that this is the bed of none other than Aubrey Carter- Ellis’ lover who she had met while closing the Deadelus Station portal in space! With this, she leaves a sign outside in case he shows up so they don’t surprise him, and the team beds down with Echo telling the disturbing bedtime story of what the walls seem to show. It looks like the temple marks the arrival of magic, the coming of the bugs, a desperate fight to contain them, and the use of some terrible weapon to send them off, and the dwindling of magic as a result. It seems this may be a 2nd or 4th World equivalent relic for the Grey Lands, and could be both the source of the ichor lake and part of the explanation for why the Awakening never happened fully in that world.

They awake, somewhat rested, but hungry and thirsty since Echo has got them on a tight rations schedule, to find Aubrey Carter sitting in his flesh-melded spacesuit, with a weapon ready but not trained on them. he recognizes Aradia, and seems to put together who Heat and Otters are from some level of contact from Ellis. They converse a bit, and he confirms that they aren’t corrupted yet, and the temple keeps them safe. He explains that he and other scouts here call these bug-less artifact areas “xenosites” and speculates that they are sort of like pearls in Vhortas (the team is horrified to have confirmed that they are in the Hive, proper, and not merely some bug incursion on another place). Carter explains that there is some speculation that once bugs are able to convert enough of the biomass of a plane to their control, they are able to fully absorb it, expanding the Hive to encompass it, but that small poison areas such as this may end up contained and empty, making them “safe” for other explorers to rest and restock, though he warns them against assorted dangers they were unaware of and encourages them to stay in the top chamber only.

In exchange for smuggling out some research data to Ellis, he offers to get them some loaner Encounter Suits, which he says should give them at least ok odds of making it off the plane with his assistance, and tells them to stay put for a day while he goes to make arrangements. They do so, spooked by his warnings, though Heat’s nose for paydays gets him to get Ruxpin to start chipping some stone flakes off to bring back, until Otters and Aradia warn against the unknown dangers of doing so. the compromise ends up being that Otters takes some careful samples and seals them well with her new chemistry kit, and Aradia carries them in her biofiber smuggling pocket. Heat makes a note to get a cyber-upgrade version of that, also partially in memory of Creep.

After what seems like around a day, Carter returns with light Encounter Suits in tow, though he explains that Ruxpin will have to shift back to human form to fit, and the Mule drone gets laden with everyone’s heavier armor.Echo is also increasingly worried about their lack of ShadowCloak, as though they are experiencing relative time in concert with the others, their internal chronometer is drifting further and further off. Suited up, and with an explanation of the protective systems and the DESPERATE need not to get any punctures, the team heads out, with Carter leading them in a sneaky-peaky. They get past some worker drones up a few tunnels, cutting through an area where two different species are repairing opposite sides of some battle damage between them, reminding them of the feeling of endless war they trudged through in the Grey Lands the day before.

Unfortunately, they get spotted trying to bypass some more alert Wasp warriors, and switch to a Bounding Overwatch, laying down grenades and suppressive fire behind them as they flee, dodging stingers left and right and trying to avoid getting cornered or stuck in a direct fight. They emerge onto what seems like a surface of sorts, though it is still a sickly green/yellow light over everything. They see some horrid clouds surrounding the upper ties of some massive termite towers, and race towards those, with Carter carrying Aradia over his beefy shoulder as she struggles to keep up, and Otters having to slow her roll riding the Mule so she doesn’t get too far out ahead. The drones are looking even worse, with their flesh twisted and dripping, taking on some of the characteristics of the more hideous flesh forms.

Reaching the base of the tower, the Wasps break off pursuit as they come under fire from termite shamans in the tower, who seem to be reshaping and using the very materials of the Hive to attack with. Aradia quickly overcasts a massive levitation spell and gets them airborne, zipping them up past the termites and weaving wildly to avoid incoming fire as they lay down suppressive fire with help from Carter at ground level. Ruxpin, with his excellent nose, smells cleaner air somewhere ahead, and directs Aradia towards it, with Aradia also seeing the terrible background count abating a bit in that direction. Finding a tiny strip of floating land in the disgusting clouds, leading to what looks like a cave mouth floating in nothing, they strip off their suits and drop them back down to Carter as promised and dash through.

Although they all enter together, they are disturbed when Echo doesn’t emerge with them. They find themselves facing a large air-shipped, manned by spirits, with a large number of flamethrowers pointed at the charred sky island they have found themselves upon. Explaining quickly that they are peaceful travellers and not bugs or cultists, they agree to submit to testing and to travel under guard to the Spire where they will be required to receive a “letter of Marque” in order to freely travel with their degree or weaponry around this place. After about 20 minutes, Echo snaps in next to them, seemingly caught up from their time dilation, and helps close out the negotiation after catching up on the current situation at hand.

The team is brought on the ship one at a time and carefully assensed under guard for any trace of bug corruption, but it seems like the strange temple and the Encounter Suits worked, and none is detected- a massive relief. After confirming that the bear (Ruxpin quickly shifted back when out of Suit) is sapient but the drones are not, the captain of the vessel, an Air spirit, takes some reagent payment for their transport, and some further bribes for some intel. They have indeed reached the Place of Clouds! So they can follow up on their mission to Arleesh, and based on the number of material plane corps and orgs represented here, they think should have an easy enough time getting home. The Captain fills them in on some local geography and threats, including sky pirates and the Great Storm, and confirms some of the other rumors they were warned of by Kendra de Santos on Zecorp.

Pulling into the Spire, they see a massive floating city set into a huge cumulonimbus cloud bank, with airship docks extending up to a kilometer out in various direction, allowing for a lot of trade and transit. They Guardian vessel that brought them in drops them off but doesn’t escort them further, as they need to get back to their post, guarding against bug intrusions. Heat and Otters are both delighted to find a functional, familiar matrix, emanating from the material plane embassy district. It seems many planes are represented with embassies here, as it is a major trade and transit hub, including Faerie, Beasts, and all the Elemental types. They head straight for “their” area, and decide to first check in with the ASPS, finding a helpful spirit whose names seems fairly unpronounceable, and agrees to go by “Ai”.

Ai proves quite helpful, though it seems like the ASPS presence here is mostly informational without a ton of resources. However, they do accept nuyen! So Echo pays for a resupply, and they arrange to get a Letter of Marque through the ASPS to shut down some of the dangerous resource extraction happening here, which lines up nicely with their job for Arleesh. They also get intel on what all of the Big Ten corps are up to here- all present to various degrees, but with vastly different MO’s and ares of operation. They eventually decide to go through Horizon to secure an airship, planning to perhaps call in favors and/or do some work for them in exchange, as well as possibly seeking out other side jobs while they’re here. Ai provides a helpful catalogue of airships, and they start shopping, but are disturbed to hear that the Disians have a presence here as well.

With Ai also restocking their travel supplies, and assorted kiosks, food stands, restaurants, and lodging around, they prepare to rest a bit and then get back to work.

Zecorp to Caelum
12/9/2024, 11:42:03 AM
Runners

The team continues their meeting with Barkins. He passes Aradia a wrapped bundle, saying it was sent at her request, and she explains to the team that it is her weapon focus, a special artifact blade from Seattle. Everyone thinks this is cool, with Aradia explaining that due to it’s ability to compel spirits, she didn’t think it would be safe or appropriate to carry in Denver. Heat gets distracted, noticing some biomechanical go-gangers checking out his parked bike out front. He and Ruxpin step outside to see if it’s going to be trouble, passing Kendra de Santos (Arleesh’s Voice), as she enters.

Out front, Heat confirms that he rides the bike and not the bear, but successfully lies his way out of participating in a race. He does this in a mildly embarrassing way though, and the go-gangers all spew exhaust out of their pipes and bio-ports and sphincters as they exit, and he and Ruxpin see Heat’s bike de-rez a bit and get actively “less cool” in response. Inside, Aradia and Echo converse with de Santos, while Otters observes it all. KDS wants them to take on another job for Arleesh/Grey Cell while they are on the metaplanes, if they can fit it in.

Apparently an MCT extraction crew has gone rogue and offered up their latest haul to the highest bidder, which is how GC got wind of it. The team is to head to the Place of Clouds (a hyperplane of the Plane of Air), and intercept this shipment, as well as fending off any other buyers. This will allow GC to analyze what MCT has been up to in their metaplanar extraction efforts, as well as who they might be selling to. She also shares the dossier they have on the Place of Clouds, giving the team a solid briefing on what to look (and look out) for there. They agree, and negotiate for more GC favors as pay, and KDs agrees, giving them two of the strange alt Tarot that seem associated with Zecorp “for luck.”

With Heat and Ruxpin reentering, the team recognizes the two cards from the team file and decides to activate one of them, the Wheel of Fortune, immediately. They each feel themselves infused with some extra luck as the card separates into 6 fragments and they each consume their slice, with one spare stashed for later. KDS bids them farewell for now, but arranges to meet with them again via Barkins on their return, or make contact at The Spire, the lone city in the Place of Clouds. The team head out towards PortHub, the hacker bar, where they hope to get intel on how to connect with Corpworld, the Resonance Realm that Perri wants them to try to contact.

Heat is a little embarrassed to be walking his bike, so Otters hops on his bike and starts rigging it, doing a slow motion wheelie alongside the walking team while Heat clings on to both bike and Otters’ VR-locked body. The passers-by don’t seem impressed, and maybe a little annoyed, but noone fucks with them, possibly due to the armored bear with a melee-hardened sniper rifle. They reach the bar and all enter, with everyone (except Ruxpin, who just walks in) successfully striking a cool pose as they are stylishly backlit on entry. Looking around, they see various styles of hacker, with a heavy emphasis on the retro and the biomechanical again.

Echo heads to the bar and is initially disgusted and rebuffed by the bartender, who seems to be most of the behind the bar equipment, sliding a torso along a long sinuous body tied into the various taps and terminals. The bartender lures Echo back with an offer of Celephais Dreamwine, which Echo accepts, and Otters also requests upon hearing this exchange. They both have a strange trance-like dream that lasts much longer for them than for their team-mates, with their ShadowCloaks degrading somewhat but protecting them from the worst of the time dilation. They do both gain some level of insight, though Echo’s is more full-fledged, as Otters’ chosen emotional limitations prevent full engagement.

Aradia and Heat chat up some hackers, with Heat impressing them with his relatively advanced skills and tricks, as well as his TM abilities, which seem rare here, though not unheard of. They get some more intel on the MCT TRU-13 teams with the captive TM’s, who seem to be seeking crossings between the metaplanes and Resonance as well. They also hear tell of some strange “techno-magicians” who accompany teams of “baldies” when they are seen on the plane- based on the descriptions of one of these techno-magicians who ran away and shifted form a few times in doing so, they think this may be where Ersatz/The Refugee escaped from- and that he was indeed working for/with Disians prior to his escape. The timeline checks out, so they plan to follow up on this upon their return.

They also hear some rumors about hackers, deep in corpo mainframes, finding strange and dangerous passageways to unfamiliar realms, as well as a lot of hackers who’ve gone missing of late. This certainly sounds like this could be either a nascent Foundation or a Reso Realm connection, so the team decides to investigate further. Based on the team file, they know ZCorp corpo’s will often trade for info on what their Earth compatriots have been up to, as potential hints to their future fortune. They decide to make an approach to an ARES/MARS exec, based on all they know about Detroit etc.

Echo tries to trade the bartender for more Dreamwine before they leave, but it is too precious, so they trade one of their bottles of gift wine for some kind of rich, diesel and blood-y spirit made locally. The team head’s to the MARS office nearest them, and talk their way in, with Echo ultimately presenting another gift bottle, and sharing a fancy liquor toast from a bottle from yet another plane known as “The Land Beneath the Sea”, that the exec bought from sandship trader. Echo secures Heat (monitored) access to the MARS mainframe host, and they head down to the sub-basement, disturbed to see that the local spiders seem to be melded into the machine.

One of the Spiders accompanies Heat and Ruxpin, who volunteers to wear a trade net and go along. He quickly has to leave as he is completely unable to navigate or make sense of the matrix. Aradia swaps in, and they explore the old-school node-based Host, with Heat eventually finding a strange anachronistic door back by their data stores. With the Spider confirming it isn’t one of theirs, Aradia distracts the Spider with seduction while Heat opens the door. The team outside sees one of the meat faces melded into the framework Host blushing from the seduction. Heat is overcome with a strange, rich, but unfamiliar Resonance connection.

This seems like the promised proof, and indeed Heat has a connection request waiting from T3chK1d, who seems equally astonished and enthused to have helped prove Perri’s UnITy theory correct. T3chK1d says perhaps they should be called “dataplanes” instead of Resonance Realms, and offers to help Heat get on Jackpoint based on this, plus is excited to work with Heat on the “Rate My Johnson” board update alongside SPYDer and others. With that, Heat closes the door, and previously seduced/distracted Spider starts taping it off til the corp can figure out what’s going on, as all Heat will say is that it isn’t a threat. They exit the matrix, and Heat finds another alt Tarot card in his pocket- the Sun, aka The Eclipse.

Heat initially wants to save this one to sell, as they review the team file and find that is has prophetic powers. The rest of the team votes to use it, with Otters especially eager to get a glimpse of the future. Given her self-centeredness, when activated, the card’s future vision focuses on Project:ANIMUS stuff, based on Otters’ interest. She luckily has become quite skilled at arcana, and interprets the dream vision fairly accurately, she thinks. It shows her two potential paths forward. In one, Horizon comes to control the ANIMUS technology, and Otters sees it’s use expanding deeper and deeper into the shadows, as Horizon uses it to further their own aims and quiet control.

In the other, Otters sees her, and various of the team in different potentialities, in NYC sometime a few months in the future, engaged in a variety of forms of intrigue and shadow biz, seemingly getting to make some choices that could effect the future of ANIMUS. When she snaps back from the vision, she very transparently refuses to tell the team everything and asks Aradia to go the ladies room with her. They find a gender-neutral bathroom to share, and Otters fills Aradia in a bit. Aradia is on-board for future NYC jobs, as the vision showed Gabrielle al-Thani as being there. They return to the team and tell them they have some fancy an exciting jobs in NYC in their future, though Otters decides not to trust/warn Heat with the Hype and Horizon intel.

They head out, and head next to the Prime Software listed address they can find online- Heat is able to remotely access stuff in the growing Noosphere in ways that Otters and the others can’t even detect. They once again talk their way in, this time asking for a software demo. The mid-level sales exec they speak with gives them access to a closed system to test it, and Heat sneakily touches it to gain broader access while also plugging in his commlink, as a cover. Echo and Aradia distract the exec with sales negotiations while Heat hacks in. He sees that their security software is indeed quite high-quality, and a bit unfamiliar, and could indeed potentially have originated off-plane.

He gets some files copied related to the creation of the software and related matters, and jacks out, since he has no idea how or what Grid Overwatch would be like here. The team get’s Prime’s CEO’s contact after conning the sales rep a bit further about the potential for a big cross-planar sale, and head out. Examining the files, they find that the devs on the software are all listed as Caelum natives, and that all their pay is sent to a single unlisted address on Zcorp- maybe where the gateway is? They head there, sending Morticia ahead to scout, and noticing that with no day/night cycle they will have to pay a bit of attention to when they will need rest- in this case, soon.

They spot a decent number of guards and cameras, and only one way in or out. They also see a CEO-style car in the fenced lot, with a fused in chauffeur face in the extended front end grille. Ruxpin gives a tactical breakdown of hitting it, and they decide to first try a peaceful approach. Heat calls up the CEO’s contact they have, and they get a slicked back hair guy in a fancy suit. After he almost gives them the brushoff, the team changes their approach and gets down to business with what they actually want, as well as what they know. The CEO seems intrigued, and tells them to come meet up at their private lab, and seems amused when they arrive about two seconds later, since they were just around the corner.

He ushers them in and Otters and Aradia notice that he seems to be a material plane denizen, posing as a ZCorp corpo. Wondering how he ended up here, Aradia briefly converses about his in-corp connections, and confirms he is a relative of Armando Salazar, though is perhaps a little out of favor or views himself as such, given this assignment. He seems to view the team as a potential tool to advancement, and though the team doesn’t feel they can trust him, exactly, they feel they can trust his avarice. He shows them the machine, which he claims is a full-on sci-fi teleporter.

The team is glad they didn’t storm the place, as it looks to take a team of 15 scientists to run and calibrate it all. Otters, Heat and Aradia all examine it and can’t quite tell if it is magitech posing as sci-fi or if this is something entirely new. Regardless, they agree to the deal- transport to Caelum and the item they seek, in exchange for other weapons they will acquire for Prime. The CEO doesn’t want to lose this deal, so pressures them into going immediately instead of resting, which they eventually do. They find themselves strapped into chairs inside a concrete sphere, with high-tech wires, tubes, circuits, lights and the like running all around.

Lasers start to play across them as they find themselves staring down an infinite light tunnel. Most of them manage alright, though Ruxpin is a bit overwhelmed. They snap forward in an instant, or maybe forever, and find themselves strapped into similar chair, but in a wholly different room, all made of perfectly smooth metal and shiny white plastic. A four-legged, four-armed figure is patiently waiting as they gather themselves, with Otters also noticing that her drones have transformed again to match the paradigm, now truly looking horrific as their odd ZeCorp forms seem to have been seamlessly wrapped in metal and plastic. That said, they seem to be running incredibly well. StepMAMA however seems to be having some trouble, letting out an agonized digital scream before rebooting.

The figure in the middle, after an appropriate time, introduces itself as Legatum, or Ambassador if they prefer, which Echo is delighted by until they realize they’ve lost their adept abilities, which bums them out. Aradia also tries to cast a spell and can’t even access mana, feeling like the drain hits but then doesn’t actually effect her. She also sees all her reagents, foci, etc are inert though intact. Also stressful. Legatum explains that in their perfectly logical world, they have eliminated magic by scraping their biosphere clean some time ago. When the team tries to inquire further, Legatum states that this is unfortunately from a time in history before their current advanced record keeping, so details are light.

They get the rest of the briefing- most denizens of the plane are genderless and use “it” pronouns, though a variety of others are also common. Violence has been edited out of their reality, which the team confirms, becoming more stressed as their weapons and attack “work” but do no damage, and the only pain they feel is the momentary learned response before their nerves catch up and report no actual damage. This further stresses them out, especially Ruxpin, who as a combat specialist feels a little useless. He also notes that he is unable to shift form, seemingly due to the absence of magic.

They ask a lot of hypotheticals, as Echo can’t quite conceptualize how a violence-free society could function. Heat is also distressed to find that he is unable to comprehend the local matrix, even though he can sort of detect it. Otters and the others can’t even see it, and Legatum explains it is an order of magnitude more advanced than their, so they shouldn’t try to interact with it. More bad news. Also, since the locals have all taken more perfect forms, there is no need for food, drink, or sleep. Legatum encourages them to remove their ShadowCloaks and join in, but they refuse, so Legatum tells them it’s good they brought supplies, and that they can rest wherever they like and everyone will move around or over them, as they can’t be harmed.

The team all hunkers down, with some of them crashing from drugs as well, which some of the locals seem to find quite repellent, and presumably illogical. They awaken and regroup, and Legatum finds them again to continue the tour, taking them around the pristine, sterile planes of sci-fi metal and plastic towers, temples and the like, explaining the plane is a place of knowledge and learning. As they try to steer the convo towards where to seek weapons, Legatum warns them that that is a taboo and not to speak of it. Otters asks after intel on mind alteration, and is directed off towards a library dedicated towards biologicals, while the rest head to the Temple of Knowledge, the largest structure at the dead center of the plane, where the Archive is apparently kept, hoping to find something there.

Otters gets some intel on mind tech, and heads back to rejoin the team. Meanwhile, the team has inquired after Null Sect, Tan Robes, and some other things, and found that the plane has little info in magical or EI threats, as they view themselves as safe from them, and also have only recently re-established outside contact with Zecorp, and thereby secondhand contact with the Material Plane. After their inquiries are rebuffed by a few more archivists, the head archivist, a horrifying mechanical centipede, comes and brings them to it’s office, introducing itself as Scriniarii. It seems fascinated by violence and unconcerned about sharing dangerous weapons knowledge with them, as they won’t be able to use it here anyway. It seems to view them as primitives, but be very interested in what they know.

They eventually agree to allow it to brainscan them, with assurances that the scans will be anonymized and not be publicly accessible for over a decade. Scriniarii says it is a nearly lossless process, but doesn’t specify to whom, and the team mostly emerges unscathed, though some of Ruxpin’s military training seems to have vanished. They do however get a copy of the Discidium as promised, as well as some trash programs, which Scriniarii derides as low-tech placeholders to tide Prime over, a mean joke which it seems to enjoy participating in. Heat is also able to get a little more info about the plane’s past.

Apparently, after some largescale die-off (the team is assuming the most recent Scourge/Horror attack), the less-advanced machine life here managed to make some kind of deal with another entity to get rid of the rest of the bio-life (which they viewed as violent liars), and start building their “perfectly logical utopia” that the team sees before them now. The team is all feeling pretty ready to leave this place, and hopefully get all their abilities back, so they say goodbye to this weird creep in the archive basement, and head out to seek Legatum and get sent back.

Deeper into the Mountain
12/2/2024, 1:13:01 PM
Runners
Otters aka Roadkill

The team, deciding not to attempt negotiations, launches into a gunfight with the Wendigo and their horde of feral ghouls. Otters in particular is surprised to see that there are two Wendigos as they usually work alone. With some careful use of suppressive fire, Heat and Otters are able to start slowing the ghoul hordes, though some ill-aimed grenades from both send the ghouls charging forward in two large groups on either side of the bone altar at the center of the chamber. Echo, setting their shotgun to a wide choke, starts thinning the herd with some well-targeted blasts, as Otters uses her Mule drone arm and winch to drop her body over the edge of the platform, suspended “safely” in the chasm.

One of the Wendigo’s charged Ruxpin after a near miss with his sniper rifle, and Ruxpin puts his melee expertise to good use, deflecting and riposting a series of devastating blows from the muscly adept. The other Wendigo, seemingly a mage, launches spell after spell at Ruxpin and the drones, but between Otters’ piloting skills and Aradia’s counter-spelling, they have little effect. Unfortunately, with Aradia as the lone mage left, her spells seem to suffer the same fate, so she focuses on leadership while levitating above the fray to avoid combat. Heat and the Lynx drone get swarmed by ghouls, but with the horde already significantly thinned out, both are able to muster their defenses and avoid harm (and hopefully infection!) while the rest of the team picks off the ghouls piled on them.

With the ghouls dealt with, the team all open up on the Wendigo locked in tense combat with Ruxpin, finally killing her. The other Wendigo goes invisible and tries to flee, but they gun her down as well before she can get far. Checking over Ruxpin and reviewing her knowledge of HMHVV strains, Otters is delighted that his armor seal is still mostly intact, and that Wendigo’s work more like vampires in terms of transmission. Heat gets a quick once over as well but in luckily uninjured, so it seems like the team got away with it this time. Ruxpin goes to investigate where the sorcerous Wendigo was fleeing, and finds a chest of “normie” disguises, most likely for the Wendigo’s to use when they had to travel to town or around un-Infected, and also find some brochures advertising a “safe and secure place” that can take care of feral ghouls- seemingly the basis of their cult here, if that’s what it was.

Heat and some of the others start to feel a little badly and wish they had tried talking first, but too late now. They debate trying to claim a Wendigo bounty with any of the groups that offer those, but realize it would be bad OPSEC if this is to be a new Nexus base, so sideline that plan for now. They also find the predated remains of various hikers and cavers who seem to have either stumbled upon this group of come close enough to get easily grabbed, and break down the bone altar into some saleable reagents, which they plan to send to Magician to maintain their good standing with that Court faction. They rest for a while, while Ruxpin and Otters (via drones) scouts the surrounding cave system. It actually is pretty good for their purposes: one small tunnel exit high on the mountainside, other paths closed off with stacked rocks but could be permanently demo’ed or opened up as needed.

They head back to the tunnel junction, and head down the other chasm branch, where Aradia detected Unseelie vibes. Feeling, more than hearing, some strange music, Heat starts to dance along with it, though most of the rest of the team resists. Ruxpin is also caught by the song, and they are both lured forward along a quite perilous path, seemingly without realizing how dangerous some of the steps, handholds, and jumps they are using truly are. The rest of the team tries to convince them to stop or slow down, but they are ignored, and quickly lose sight of Heat and Ruxpin around a bend in the tunnel. Those two continue ahead, quickly finding themselves joining a chaotic throng of dancers, all moving to odd, unfamiliar music, seemingly eminating from a castle at the center of what looks to be a medieval-ish town.

They both dance along, joining the crowd, pausing occasionally as their muscles spasm and cramp to drink from some open barrels of some kind of sweet mead scattered around. The rest of the team, worried and unsure what to do, continues advancing slowly. Aradia summons an air spirit to go try to break Heat out of it, who reports back via their astral link about there being some kind of metaplanar crossing ahead, and is itself quickly lost to the dance once it enters and finds Heat. Aradia releases it from service but it seems full under the spell, and remains dancing.

As they reach the crossing, they see an almost seamless transition from tunnel to town, but can see it is all rotted and ruined, strewn with the long and recent dead, as dancers fall away from exhaustion, with fresh ones hurrying to join the queue. The barrels of mead are turned, and sickeningly sweet, and the dancers seem unaware of dangers around them, dancing off the tops of ruined towers as they try to take illusory steps, falling through holes in ruined floors and bridges, etc. The “music” sounds more like the wind whistling through ruined structures and the stamping of feet than anything pleasing. However, Otters start to see the appeal of it all, just for a moment, before Echo gets StepMAMA to blast loud static into everyone’s in-ears, with Aradia commanding them to return.

This snaps Otters out of it, and Heat comes to partially through the pain of dancing so hard that he has injured himself. They end up having to knock Ruxpin out and levitate him back, but they escape and get back to the tunnel junction, though Aradia feels bad leaving the air spirit behind. They decide that since nothing follows them, this could actually be a good security feature, with the right precautions taken, and then everyone spends a little while trying to dissuade Heat from incorporating too much of what he heard there into his music, which he seems very interested in doing. They rest for a spell, and then realizing that there drugs are starting to wear off, they head back to the van and field hospital.

With Aradia and Heat crashed on Novacoke, and Ruxpin injured, Echo does some careful management of personalities to get a reasonable camp set up, and they spend a chilly night, with Otters needing some supplemental oxygen in the high altitude. The next morning, they head back, and follow the manufactured tunnel past the junction this time. They shortly find a large, intact vault door, which Heat and Otters spend some time tinkering with to apply power and bypass the mechanical locks before convincing it to finally open. Inside, they find a musty but intact poured concrete bunker, stripped of any paydata similar to the field hospital, but with some (decades-a century old) survival equipment left behind, and plenty of space.

After scouting it thoroughly, they don’t find any other access points, so this seems perfect. They find a few cars for cargo and passengers that fit the train tracks, but there’s still no power and enough breaks in the lines that it will be some work to get them up and running. They write up a report as they hike back out, with Heat occasionally making everyone uncomfortable with some jokes about dancing. They reach the surface, cover the “secret” entrance back up, and drive out of the mountains, with Heat reporting in to a very pleased Perri about what they found. He spends some time composing music based on what he remembers hearing versus the sounds recorded on the TacNet, and sends it Richie Macey to see what effect it has.

Macey report that he’d be interested to hear more, as it is certainly unique, but that he doesn’t quite “get it” as of yet. Heat tries to recruit Aradia into helping compose it more accurately, alongisde a Music Sprite, but gets talked out of it for the time being, though he plans to check in on Macey in a day or two to see if there were any lingering effects. Macey sends along some new rebel country stuff he’s working on about conflicted feelings about a new AAA corp in your region- more money, also more problems as it turns out. The team rests back at the safehouse and then stocks up on gear for the metaplanes, including new or upgraded shadow cloaks, restocked survival kits, and the like. Heat and Otters help mod Ruxpin’s rifle to work in his bear form, removing the trigger guard and hardening it, as well as increasing the size of some of the components, so Ruxpin can go to the planes in bear form more comfortably. Ruxpin confirms he is not to be ridden, though he will haul teammates out of danger as needed.

With Arcane approving large drones and small vehicles for transit through the portal he will prepare, Heat decides to bring his motorcycle, and Otters brings a decent collection of drones. They drive to a rural crossing in the southern FRFZ and meet Arcane, and Aradia is excited to feel some tracings of Quimbanda in the choice of location. They all hang and chat a bit while Otters remote pilots the van back to the safehouse. Echo impresses Arcane with a gift and butters him up a bit, which he seems to appreciate, and the team also fills him in on their strange recent astral experience. He tells them that they were lucky to survive an encounter with Reproach, a fallen Court of the Wild Fae, which has been overcome with something known as the “Dancing Sickness” or the “Dancing Plague” which often proves lethal to visitors and those caught in it’s spell, for the purpose of endlessly sustaining the courtly fun of the lord of the realm and his courtiers, who flit from body to body as they fall.

Fargus Sol also reports in from Grey Cell, with some updates on McClure, the AZT exec they helped extract in Seattle. They’ve been debriefing him extensively, and AZT has been moving hard to take him back, levelling a not insignificant portion of a few cities and town near places he was rumored to be, without success so far. Lots of AZT experts in the shadows (JP etc) have been going dark or reporting hit squads coming after them (if they survive). At the same time, bounty boards start posting a lot of high paying gigs with hacker targets. It seems AZT is worried about what kind of accesses and backdoors McClure may have set up before his exit, as he apparently also took a large sum of funds with him through some clever accounting. One early piece of intel is that AZT has been devving tech to mess with the metaplanar barrier, likely using help from Dis. The AZT/Dis relationship seems to go back over a decade at least, which predates any known contact besides the First Expedition to the Seelie Court.

Arcane wants to know more about the access point to Reproach, so they give Perri his info, and she says she’ll get in touch with him about consulting on magical security since he has a good rep, so long as he agrees to the Nexus’ terms on security protocols for visiting the site etc. With that done and the van finally parked, Arcane smoothly opens a portal into a familiar low-lying alley in the Zecorporatum sprawl, and the team heads through, with Heat wheeling his bike for now since the others aren’t mounted. The team sees the bike, alongside the Mule drone and Morticia, start to conform to the local biomechanical paradigm, though their shadow cloaks protect them once again. They take a look (and smell) around, also in the matrix and assensing. Heat notices that Perri seems to be onto something- the Noosphere has notably expanded here since their last visit, and in ways that only Heat can detect- so it isn’t full wireless matrix, but is a sort of Resonant energy generated by technology, it seems.

They also, more upsettingly, see some Null-like vines creeping in from a few open sewer grates, cracked windows, and the like, always kind of at edges, in dead-end alleys, and the like. They do spot one team in containment suits, with 5 armed individuals seemingly controlling or sort of steering a 6th. Heat gets a faint warning from that 6th figure to “stay back or they’ll get you to” and Otters recognizes the style of containment suit as belonging to MCT’s TRU-13 esoteric research division, so they assume this is some MCT black op involving a kidnapped TM. Heat wants to help but holds off for the moment, wanting to get a better sense of things here first. They head to Batholomew Barkins, Esquire’s office, and contract with him get some local intel and leads, as well as to leave the plant-cat spirit that Arcane sent along at his office. This way, they can (hopefully) easily track down the plantcat again when they are ready to contact Arcane and get portaled home.

Paying Barkins in reagents after some negotiation, they get intel on a local hacker bar to check for info on possible connections to Corpworld, where Heat is supposed to try to contact a TM named T3chK1d to prove another theory of Perri’s. They also get some intel on the vines/MCT, though MCT (as with all the corps) seems to have a slightly variant local presence. And finally/most importantly, they get a lead on tracking down the Discidium- a local AzCorp subsid called Prime Software apparently sells security ‘ware that they claim is built by thinking machines, which Barkins hasn’t seen or heard of locally, so assumes must be some secretive off-place contact they have. This seems like a good lead, and Barkins reminds them that ZCorp is a great planar crossroads, so they have high hopes of finding the things they seek without too much trouble, and head out into the ever-present gloaming of the plane, the rain-slick street shining with the reflected lights of the enormous tiers of towers all around them.

Ruxpin drops Echo off and hurries to get Heat over to join the fight as combat begins….
