The team links back up, with PPJ having relaxed in his downtime, and TD having met with Rai, the Court detective, and learned about the Nightwatch Phantom, as well as agreeing to collab further on an investigation into the Ghost Breeder’s mysterious husks. Everyone’s feeling just about ready to leave, but unclear where to go next- Mile High Realm, back to Denver, or off further on more metaplanar quests? For now they decide to tie up loose ends around the Castle and plan to leave the following day after the Morning Hearing.

Creep, bored of libraries, declares his intentions to chill today, and stays in their Magician quarters in the form of a chair, watching flatvids. Heat and Aradia head off to track down Samantha Roth, and find her in the corp office area, where they pass along Null/Geocache info in exchange for getting a one-time use commcode to contact Celedyr when they are back on Earth, in order to arrange for use of the Jewel of Memory in freeing Harelquin. She warns them that he will require something of them in return. They also arrange to involve her in their plan to prevent further Null presence at Court, if it works out.

They next head out the castle’s gates to seek Warden Banleigh Swale, the groundskeeper, and track him down after following the sounds of screaming. Unfortunately, Aradia doesn’t dodge his warning quickly enough and is paralyzed by a Mandrake’s cry. Heat dives out of the way and is summoned back as the screaming cuts off, to find Swale removing large earmuffs and tutting disapprovingly at Aradia’s state. Aradia, a little freaked out, decided to astrally project, and does so, appearing almost as if manifesting next to her body. However, she seems to be able to move around, though not at the usual speeds, and can communicate but only use astral spells- strange….

Heat manages to eventually convince the cantankerous Swale to check out the Null vine problem, and then hauls Aradia’s body back into the castle alongside him, planning to hit up Magician’s alchemists for a cure. Aradia remains in astral form, floating alongside them. They track down a page while seeking the alchemist, and message Roth to meet them at Geocache. With Aradia restored, they all meet at the strange chamber, and Swale is initially quite put off by it’s oddities. Heat also sees another Crow observing their activities here. After Swale says he needs to see the problem to assess it, Heat cracks open the NN Star again, revealing the Null vines bursting forth to grasp them.

He quickly slams it shut again, but sees that Swale has summoned a large glowing magical weapon in shock. He agrees this is an issue, and starts putting up ornately embroidered “caution tape” around it, struggling a bit at where to pound stakes into the green and black wireframe grid. Heat tries to convince Swale to be more aggressive with it, but Roth takes Swale’s side, ingratiating herself, on containment being the best option for now. This seems to serve their general interest in allying with Roth, so Heat lets it go and they call it a win overall. They start heading back towards the safehouse to make evening plans.

Meanwhile, Aradia, Otters and TD have begun their investigation into the Nightwatch Phantom, seeking the Castle roof and finding it without much trouble. A rusting pile of armor and shields sits next to a narrow stair that emerges through a hatch onto a mostly flat roof, with some pitches, domes, skylights, etc.A thick fog also coats the roof and moves without wind, blocking views further than a few meters in any direction for long. They strategize a bit and begin discussing the NP out loud, which seems to summon it- they have found the area of it’s Haunt!

They all hear thudding steps moving towards them, but seemingly approaching from different directions as they close in. Otters hears a large sword scraping across stone, and turns just in time to fire a full auto burst with the drone before it is sliced in half by the sword. The NP seems to almost ignore the damage, it’s flame-blackened armor emitting occasional flickers as if it burns from within and the impression of a melted face that Otters can’t quite retain. The NP swings again, and forces TD to dive back in defense. TD tries to explain that they are trying to help, assuming the NP doesn’t want to continue haunting, but comes under attack again, as does PPJ as he assenses it, and tries to haul Otters’ bod to safety.

Luckily PPJ’s armor soaks the heavy hit he receives, and they all manage to dive down another nearby hatch, PPJ having seen a thin astral trail leading that way- maybe the Ghost Chain for the NP? They find themselves adjacent to another pile of rusted armor, which Otters stares at, paralyzed with fear. She notices that though the components are piled haphazardly and poorly maintained, some of them could be reassembled into a suit resembling the NP. She starts making specs to do so as PPJ fumbles about trying to help. They all take cover in the armor pile as the NP reappears, blocking their exit, but seems not willing to further damage the armor.

TD proves much more adept at reassembly, and also starts polishing and lubing it up, with Otters joining in once her fear clears up a bit. Once they armor is put back together, some of the dings and dents pounded out, and it polished and lubricated as best they can, TD offers up the possible resolution to the NP, who gives a cross forehead sword salute and then vanishes. Examining the armor, PPJ can tell the NP is inside, though it seems a bit diminished in some way, but also less aggro. TD explains that they wish to continue to help, and PPJ does a Death Replay spell, gaining some insight into the NP’s living predecessor’s final moments- immolated in his armor by a Thorn Elf of Alachia’s faction, seemingly during an Unseelie attack on the Castle in the Era of the Corpse Lily.

With this, and TD managing to figure out a simple yes/no language to communicate in, they manage to convey that they are also opposed to Alachia and working against her goals, and the NP offers them some protection and then vanishes, armor and all. Creep is startled as a large armored figure appears, facing outward, guarding the door of their quarters in Magician’s dorms. He considers shooting it but it seems to be ignoring him, so he just levels his gun at it and waits. The others arrive shortly thereafter, with Aradia and Heat showing up first, and very surprised by the waiting knight. After the remaining 3 report in to Rai, they also return and explain.

Chloe Glowworm also messages them to the direct them to their new more permanent quarters, now that they have officially allied with Magician- a Courtsman’s Suite, with an individual room for each members, a small kitchenette, and a lounge area with fireplace. The NP once again disappears and reappears just inside the door to this suite as they arrive, and confirms it will guard the portal from unwanted guests. The team agrees to keep sensitive discussions on comms in the quarters, jic, and also to try to dodge the inevitable Magician surveillance they expect. They manage to track down an event for the night, a wine and poetry evening that Boutrous the Boisterous invited Aradia too, when they were conversing about the Order of the Garter.

Arriving, with Creep in tow, they find Deigh Hall, a somber and somewhat classicist chamber seemingly built to subtly mock the current Queen as herself being a bit out of date, if classy. Heat is bored stiff by the event, and struggles to conceal this fact and entertain himself. Creep greatly impresses the gathering with a poem he composed about Alachia in an ancient Croki style, which given the hall they are in, some present seem to take as mockery rather than the flirtation Creep intended, but it goes over well. Otters manages to deliver a satisfactory ode to her recently destroyed drone, and the team manages to prevent any dueling or other fallout in response to Boutrous’ legendarily bad “poems”.

With that having refreshed them all a bit (the wine and snacks were high quality, of course), they return to their Suite and all rest well, even the agitated Heat. They are a little disturbed to see what looks like a mopped up bloodstain just outside their door, and TD confers with the NP and finds that there was an attempted intruder, who took a heavy blow for their trouble but survived and fled. They head to the Morning Hearing, and see that the tapestries all seem to be telling parts of the tale of the NP, including a murder he was framed for, which the team knows was the work of the runners Sanquals Love and Chessick.

A few petitioners come before the Queen, some astral religious pilgrim seek permission to cross Fae lands while seeking entrance to Kadath- they believe they know a secret way through to a “city of eternal light” but the team senses something a bit off and sends a message mentioning this to Magician. A spirit named Kostas (who PPJ thinks might have a Black Lodge connection from back in the day in Boston) gets up on Magician’s behalf and reports the suspicion, and Chambray Cliff and some others quickly jump on it, wary of intruders, and an internal group within the pilgrims admit to being a separate sect travelling alongside them.

They ID themselves as “the Brotherhood of the Key”, and say they seek to commune something called “the Gatekeeper”. The Court seems concerned, but a member of Threshold appears unexpectedly, and offers patronage to the 3 BotK members, which they accept. They rest of the pilgrims are offered passage through the lands and suggested to hurry along and not to attempt to recruit others on their journey, as Cliff and others courtiers suggest they will surely all perish, or worse ending up beyond Kadath as further fodder for the Horrors.

An emissary of the Wild Hunt arrives and offers forth a bounty for Crawlers and other lesser Horrors that have begun to appear in the fae wilds and the Astral Sea, as well the favor of Chalcis, the Wild Hunt’s leader, to those hunters who are successful. Some of the team is pretty interested, but they try to stay on track. Lastly, the Queen announces the imposition of “the Rite of Root and Vine” on a courtier caught trying to break into a Courtsman’s Suite, and the team recognizes one of the other people who was questioned by Cliff about their involvement in the Shadow faction- why were they trying to access the team’s chambers?

They are a bit horrified to see Alachia perform the Rite, which seems to grow a thorny collar just under the skin of the courtier, the tightness of which Alachia seems able to control with a ring. The Magister explains that the courtier is banned from fae lands on pain of excruciating death by thorn suffocation, and without further ado the courtier is tossed through a summoned portal to parts unknown. Heat then jumps in line, to deliver a warning about the dangers of the Null, and does get some accolades for his presentation, but realizes he probably actually gave some useful ammo to the isolationists, who want less matrix here, not more.

Through the hearing, Alachia has been sitting on a large couch she appears to have dressed Harlequin up as, seemingly as a return slight to the poem Creep read the previous night, and Creep sees various courtiers subtly ditching elements of furniture fashion as it rapidly goes out of style. He laughs at Alachia as the Hearing finishes, but she ignores him. However, he knows he got to her, and finds the bizarre power play intriguing. With that out the way, the team updates Rai further about the NP, lets some folks know they’ll be leaving for a bit (including PPJ alerting Cliff that any lone PPJ’s he sees soon are infiltrators, which a gets a surprised nod in response), and TD stocking up on cleaning and repair supplies from some of the shops by Gilik’s.

They let Foggy know they’ll be gone a while, and she offers to let them update the Eclipse astronomer Al’Farabi about what their former teammates found on the Moon with Foggy. They agree, and head there quickly to do that before leaving, and Al’Farabi is quite interested to hear it all. She takes a mana photo of them as a thank you, to hang in their suite, and everyone is a little disturbed that it clearly shows that PPJ’s magical tongue and hands are connected to the strange crystal fused to his spine that seems to give him both occasional power and occasional direction. They discuss a bit but make not decisions for now.

The Detective splits off from the team to meet with some investigators around the Court, and Pepperjack sticks with them to make sure noone travels alone. In exchange, they give Niall a thorough debrief on what Aradia and TD saw in the Pendulum room, and he explains that there are some members of the Unseelie seeking alliances at Court for various purposes, and that this may have been such a meeting.

He also explains that some amongst the Unseelie seek darker allies- Horrors and the like, as the Courts were spared the full effects of the Scourge last time around, they seem to think this could permanently shift the balance. He explains that the Unseelie contain just as many factions and opinions as the Seelie Court, but tend to operate more in secret and are not as organized around a space. Regardless, he thinks that helping to free Harly and making a staunch ally of Aina in the process will be good for helping the more stable factions within the Unseelie to prevail, and not launching into a new period of dangerous chaos for the Courts. As Niall seems to support this overall endgoal (a more unified Unseelie under Aina) he also offers to help transport the team to a plane of their choosing when they are ready.

Aradia and Creep head off to seek the Pendulum room again, to try to get more intel for Niall. They eventually find it, but it is unoccupied. They start their own dance, with Creep in the lead, set to the rhythm of the swing of the chamber, and gradually find that they can almost feel more than hear the beat and some sense of music. Another couple headed home from a ball somewhere else joins them for a time, but noone else comes along, so they head back toward’s Magician’s dorms, but are disturbed to discover an exploded body in a narrow stair as they make their way through the Castle. Creep does some analysis, and they determine that the scraps of uniform remaining are from a dungeon guard, and Aradia is able to tell that this is definitely the Blood Shade they last fought on Carcean.

Apparently, it did indeed escape that place in the other PPJ bod that got left behind, then seems to have escaped the dungeon (as they heard whispers of) by at some point ditching that bod and swapping into a dungeon guard, and now has shed that shell, and taken up some other visage. Unless it has decided to roam free as a visible Blood spirit, but Aradia thinks this is unlikely based on the astral cleansing and high level Masking at play that keep her from finding a trail. They head back to the dorms for now, watching their backs.

The rest of the team decides to call it an early night, after making a plan for the Morning Hearing, and checks in to their Magician dorm, courtesy of Chloe Glowworm. When they all wake up the next day, she offers them longer term patronage for some ongoing cross-planar work for the faction, and they accept, ignoring the absent PPJ’s feelings and wishes on the subject. Chloe explains that their leader, Secundus, is seeking the Philosopher’s Stone, and Otters and Creep almost lose their cool at being tasked with another impossible job, til Chloe manages to get a word in edgewise and explain.

She says that they are seeking alchemists and other practitioners from Earth (or other metaplanes) with similar interests to Magician, to expand their talent pool. The team agrees, and from the file is able to recommend Squeamish from Seattle, who seems to have a similar interest in immortality. Creep’s interest is also mildly piqued that a Fae (who are maybe immortal?) would need a second option, maybe something to look into. Heat writes up a flattering introduction for Magician to send to Squeamish, and as they finally have a patron, their gear is accessible. Otters instantly unpacks her progressively more horrifying drone, and dives in. It feels good, and the drone seems to be working normally despite it’s appearance, but the lack of external matrix and freeware/OSI sensor data is a bit off-putting.

They head to the Morning Hearing and notice that the mosaics and tapestries now show a series of people exploding from Blood Shade inhabitation- recognizable to those in the know as Teeth, Aleiron, and the as yet unnamed guard. The images are sketchy enough to not be instantly id’able to someone who doesn’t know the tale, so the team decides to take advantage of their information exclusivity as a distraction, with Aradia sending a series of notes out before the Hearing properly begins. She requests a meeting with Foggy (accepted, over a light luncheon), tries to message Warden Banleigh Swale, the groundskeeper, to see if he can help with the Null Sect problem (rejected- it flits to the open door and then crashes to the ground- it seems like Swale is not present and the messages can’t leave the Great Hall), and lastly to Alachia.

This is arguably the most important message, and not wanting to cause a scene, Aradia allows the dropped message to Swale to be lost in the milling crowd of Courtiers, and presumably picked up by someone. The message to Alachia tells of the exploded guard’s location and the Blood Shade connection, and while she is distracted reading that, Otters hotrigs the dog drone to go over and start just suspiciously loitering and bumping into the tie downs on the tarp over the Shadow Tourmaline, trying to distract the Crows scattered around Court doing matrix security.

With this all seeming to work, Heat takes the opportunity to take a look around, hiding in the matrix to observe the flow of Resonant energy from Alachia’s collection of Pâ tokens to various coimeadai around the Court. He manages to elude detection, and spot 12 connected signals. He points them out to Creep, who takes over, doing careful observation of their various dense but interlinked tattoos throughout the rest of the Morning Hearing, eventually id-ing a single set of 3 amongst them, which the team gets good images of. These are likely to be the 3 that they were told Harlequin’s mind was split between, and with Niall having explained that they have multiple options for how to access the memories (basically getting a legit link to Alachia’s network, hacking it, or destroying the coimeadai themsevles) they feel like this is enough of a plan for now, as they need the Jewel of Memory and a specific plan for how to deal with Gwynplaine before they are fully ready.

There are some interesting supplicants at the Morning Hearing as well- a freelance sandship captain who roams the astral sea puts out a request for a book from the Library of Leng, on Id, to be delivered to the Violet Gas, way out on the edge of the astral sea. Serania, the Ancient One, the head of Eclipse, has sent her emissary Catondarez to offer support to Montage in his quest to find the metaplanar crossing he had sought assistance discovering, and also to offer rewards for any other new metaplanar discoveries. The team makes note for the future.

Some Catholic Sacred Detectives (consecrated investigators who cannot return from their pilgrimages until their assigned mystery is resolved) request help investigating a multi-planar serial killer they are calling “the Ghost Breeder”, who leaves empty, essence-less, but dangerous husks behind as all that remain of it’s victims. This is rejected by the Queen, who reminds the Catholics that they are not welcome at Court (in response to which Otters sees two Courtiers wearing “Blackheart Security” corp insignia looking pleased) due to the Church’s meddling in the “rightful Fae rulership of Tir na nOg”. She thanks them for their warning of this potential danger but her Champion confirms no sign of activity has been detected at Court, and now that they have been made aware, the Sacred Detectives are sent on their way. Some of the team notice that Ashokasundari Rai, the classic detective-styled courtier, seems interested.

More disturbingly, some insect spirits who refer to themselves as “free” request sanctuary, or at least a place to rest. They explain that sometimes high level servitors will gain some level of sentience upon the death of their Queen, and offer their services to the Court for a safe place to stay, as they do not wish to be subsumed (or consumed) back into Vhortas, or to be hunted by Earth warriors of Spider spirits. The Queen agrees for now, but doesn’t grant them a permanent status and warns them to be on their best behavior. The team worries this just swapping one Queen for another, but they have no real power here so what can they do. They are also disturbed to notice that many of the corp reps visible around the chamber seem delighted to have the chance to converse with some potentially “free” Insects, as this has long been ARES’ private domain, loathe though they are to admit it.

Lastly, a Croki ambassador for the Wild Fae, Riva Dhillon, requests help with a corp strip mining project in the Wilds, a corp (likely MCT from the description) is intentionally creating toxic zones to harvest strange reagents. The Queen rejects this request, as she has not given permission for such activity nor is it occurring on her lands, so she doesn’t see how this relates to the Court. Dhillon, seemingly a skilled speaker, manages to accept this result, while deriding the Court’s narrow-mindedness, and obliquely referring to past examples of this policy going poorly for the Court and/or the Fae as a whole. With that the hearing ends, and the team heads off to a light snacks and tea lunch with Foggy. They had over two Tarot cards, a matching set, one from the 6WT, and one from one of the two other mysterious decks they have encountered. Foggy says that this is a superb gift for the Queen, and herself seems quite excited about the revelation about the existence of further decks. They worry they might have given TOO good of a gift, but Foggy suggests that due to it’s lateness, the quality will be appreciated.

In return, as Foggy basically has to ask them to ask her for something, Heat pulls out the facemask he has programmed to match The Auditor for the LA mission, and asks if Foggy recognizes him. She doesn’t, and doesn’t seem particularly interested until the team brings up some of the weird stuff him and his similar looking possible colleague Fidian Serverson (if that is even his name!) have been up to in Denver, LA and likely elsewhere. She offers to look into it more, and they agree.

With that, they have a few last threads to tie up but plan to leave the Seelie Court shortly for their meeting with Aina, and then onwards. Creep has also been excited to spot a few instances of furniture fashion popping up around Court, some upholstered fabrics, some decorative wooden wearable trim, etc. He seems to have started a mini-trend, and is curious where it will go.

My Dad is a Chair

Runners

Pepper Jack

Heat splits off from the team to do some digging into local musical culture and pass Null Sect info along to Horizon to keep the team in their good graces for another month of public news presence management. Aradia heads out to search out promising candidates for the Order of the Garter. With PPJ and TD still detained, Otters and Creep debate whether to seek out dinner arrangements or not, as they’d made no particular plans for the evening.

They decide not to, feeling that they’ve both actually been eating more than they really need to, once they consider it. They head over to Ehran the Scribe’s mysterious recruitment booth, hoping to secure a place to sleep, and then fumble their way through a conversation, lacking all of their usual Faces. Creep’s blunt style keeps almost shutting down their chances til Otters repeatedly clarifies and salvages things. After confirming that they don’t have a factional alliance yet, aren’t actually interested in his recruiting pitch (hiring specialists for a longterm assignment at location unknnown, which would involve saying your goodbye’s to everyone you know for now), and aren’t actually allied with Alachia The Blood Queen (who Creep has a possibly ill-advised crush on), Ehran shuts down his booth for a break and brings them around to his private quarters behind it, shaping open a door in the castle wall to do so.

After conversing a bit, they actually manage to impress him with the depth of their knowledge (thanks team files and Toff’s journal!) so he fills them in on some stuff and offers them some work. Frosty Jane is apparently not allied at Court, due to being out of favor (perhaps related to the Chal’Han that Toff’s team helped her in, in which she defeated Alachia’s daughter), and thus was unable to accompany him on this trip. Also, she has been doing damage control on Earth to try to keep news of Harlequin’s current situation from going public, even resorting to posing as him on Jackpoint and elsewhere using his “Laughing Man” handle. Assuming that the admins don’t know about this, this is a major faux pas. Ehran also reveals that he has posted on various of the shadow forums himself, when necessary, but suggests that his level of fame doesn’t make regular interaction with the hoi polloi the most effective means of communication, most often.

After confirming that the team knows the basics of Harly’s recent history and current deal, he says that he and Jane would like to hire some unaffiliated contractors to try to improve Harly’s situation. He explains that it will be a complicated process, and that breaking the Coimeadai bargain, though it is unlikely that Harly entered it willingly, will involve some strange and ancient magics. They will also need to collect however many pieces he has been fragmented into, including Gwynplaine, to do their best to ensure success. Ehran doesn’t know GP’s current location but thinks he is at least somewhere on the Plane. He also tells them that they will need to find some way to bargain with the great dragon Celedyr, who retreated to his fortress in Wales after the collapse of NeoNET, managing to dodge most of his share of the fallout for what he helped to set off in Boston.

Via the team files, and with Ehran filling in the gaps, they piece together that they will need the Jewel of Memory, a draconic artifact the possession of which signifies the holder as the draconic Loremaster. This was a title long held by Lofwyr, until he was dethroned during the early-70’s Dragon Civil War, when he slew his dragon-supremacist brother, Alamais. The JoM was then given to Celedyr, who managed to maintain it through the Boston aftermath, and still holds it to this day. As the team file shows, Test’s research into the Coimeadai shows that that ritual was developed either from the JoM, inspired by it, or similar, and Ehran suggests that such a power object could help un-do what has been done to Harly.

However, they will also need a powerful practitioner of Courtly magic, and one who does not mind going against one of the Queen’s closest allies, along with the edicts and traditions of Queen and Court. To that end, Ehran has secured them lodging for the evening in the mansion of Thisbe Sorrel, the Queen in Exile. He tells them to stay on their toes but also to assess her potential interest in the mission at hand. They agree, and head straight there, not wanting to risk the castle halls at night. They are ushered in by a plant-matter humanoid spirit named Walks Through Tall Grass, who studiously ignores Creep’s attempts at mild antagonism.

When they enter, they find a number of children of various ages playing, including two toddlers who bear notable resemblances to Sanquals Love and Armando Salazar, as hinted at in the team file. Creep also spots that a teen sitting in an armchair at the side of the room is none other than their host, Thisbe. Her eyes, filled with ancient knowledge and a long, long life give her away, though she fooled Otters, initially. They converse a bit, with Otters growing more and more bored and frustrated as she is reminded yet again that she STILL ISN’T IN VR! She also notices that Thisbe seems fascinated by and is aggressively flirtacious with Creep, which seems to be totally going over Creep’s head.

When Creep and Thisbe heads upstairs for her to more closely study the scarification done to him to prep him as a Shadow vessel, Otters stays behind and reads some of the various books, scrolls, tomes and tablets left scattered about. Most are in languages and scripts unfamiliar to her, but a few are legible. Amongst them she finds the spell, crafted by Thisbe, to originate the Great Hall messaging system, which just lightly uses blood magic. Otters records the spell formula to share with others later. She also finds some notes about Mama (from Boston), some past team members, and Mama making incursions into the Metaplanes. After exhausting her reading materials, she curls up on one of the divans to try desperately to sleep, but also sees that one of the children is playing with a set of oddly shaped blocks that seems to reshape the mansion around them.

Upstairs, Thisbe strips Creep down and makes thorough record of his markings. He explains he is watching Roseanne during this and she inquires if he finds this sexually stimulating. He does not, but agrees to provide her with a “sample” in exchange for her assistance, switching his internal feed to a playback of the somewhat D/s chatroom hang he had at new year’s with Dr. Tilda Aurand. This gets the job done quickly, and Sorrel says she’s head off to the bath, telling Creep to sleep “where he will.” Creep dresses, returns to the foyer, and sleeps under the divan that Otters is curled up on. They awake in the morning to one of Sorrel’s servants (/older children?), Edselia, offering refreshments, as Thisbe emerges down the stairs, visibly pregnant.

She tells them in a roundabout way that she will assist them in their task as they fumble through a few inquiries, and suggests that Heat, or another TM, should be able to trace the 3 Coimeadai that hold Harly’s mind from the Pá coins that will either be held by Alachia or one of her close minions. She also says she will help with the ritual as she seems quite interested in esoteric spellcraft, amongst other things. Her and Otters go on a brief tangent about riggable corpses, which Otters quickly abandons but she seems to wish to explore further. She then offers to perform a ritual on Creep to ensure that his body is destroyed in the event of his death, preventing it’s use as a Shadow vessel, and Creep agrees, feeling a distressingly hot burning sensation in his chest after she licks a sample of his blood off his finger and focuses on him for a moment.

The sensation fades to a pleasant but detectable warmth, and they head to the Morning Hearing with a few members of the Sorrel household, though not Thisbe herself. Not seeing Heat or Aradia there, they are happy to at least spot their detained team-mates, hauled up before the Queen’s dais. They also see that someone has casually tossed a ratty tarp over the Shadow tourmaline, seemingly done in an insultingly insouciant fashion on purpose, while still blocking it from broadcasting messages on it’s surface. And the mosaics and murals today seem focused on Queens: one Queen, upright, with a spectral double, both crowned, while another crowned Queen, inverted, watches from the shadows. Three more Queens cavort and scheme, with partial crowns or hair styled as such: one in a thorny wood, one in a glade, and one in a pool of blood. They think they can ID almost all of the figures, which also seem to boost some of the rumors travelling the court, but one of the lesser Queens remains a mystery.

Meanwhile, TD and PPJ have been attempting to maintain their sanity in their tiny, void-edged dungeon cells. PPJ also almost loses himself to the laughter, with TD providing some critical leadership to bring him back from the (literal) edge, as he all of a sudden realizes that poking his toes into the void isn’t actually that funny at all. They converse a bit, with TD trying to make sure this it the real PPJ, while both try to ignore the laughter. After an unclear amount of time passes, they are blindfolded again and brought forth from their cells, and TD overhears one of their jailors muttering something about “one of them having escaped” before being shushed. They are brought up to Court, blindfolds removed, and happy to see their friends in the audience.

The Rite of Testimony starts, with the Queen’s Magister reading the ritual from a book that the Queen’s Steward holds. TD assenses the magic to make sure no other castings are involved, and when it looks like it is those two plus some ambient Castle magic, submits, not seeing a better option. PPJ does the same. A few different Courtiers are questioned- Chambray Cliff seems to be digging into both Shadow faction activity as well as the threat of Blood doppels. From the first few questionings, it seems that Cliff is playing a complex political game and trying to score points more than make examples, although the Queen seems to be growing bored by the lack of major revelations, and the Magister and Queen’s Champion both occasionally interject in the questioning. One of the other early questioned reveals that he is there on behalf of STV to set up a law office, with permission, but initially just working on behalf of their client the Magical Investors Group. Since MIG has the Queen’s permission to study there and Foggy vouches for STV, he is quickly released.

The worst moment for Cliff is in his questioning of disgraced former High Priestess member Sacre Floot, who reveals under the Rite that she was tortured by Hermit faction or their agents. Cliff manages to shut it down quickly but that doesn’t seem to have gone quite how he liked. TD is forced to reveal their regrets over failed past cases, a private shame made public but no great secret, and they see a classic Holmsian-styled detective, an Indian woman, nodding along as if she understands. TD notes that for later. PPJ is up next, and Cliff digs deep. Creep and Otters attempt to cheer and boo at appropriate times in support of PPJ but it doesn’t quite work, so Creep turns into a chair to hide, seeing some interested looks at this display of sartorial disguise. They both back off a bit though.

It seems like the unifying factor amongst all of the Courtiers being questioned is that they received messages from the tourmaline, were seen looking at or listening to it, or volunteered themselves (TD). PPJ hands over the note the tourmaline gave him, which like the other missives is recorded into the Steward’s book but not read aloud- it seems that although the prohibition against discussing the tourmaline has been lifted for this Rite, the Queen still has some prohibitions on it in place. PPJ also reveals his backstory, missing memories and weird times on the metaplanes and all. He’s very open about everything, though Cliff keeps trying to twist his words to suit his own purposes.

Eventually, PPJ is forced to reveal his largest secret- he has a mysterious implant that he doesn’t understand, which seems to connect him to an unknown entity whose feeling seem to effect PPJ’s access or lack thereof to occasional bursts of additional power. This is a little more explicit about this than he has been all at once with the team, though he has told them all of these things bit by bit. Cliff seems to view this as the big reveal, since the crystal, as well as PPJ’s spine, are blood implants, but it doesn’t hit as hard at Court as desired and PPJ largely keeps his cool. Cliff hurries through the final Courtier, who is detained for further question after being revealed to have collected messages from the tourmaline, believing them important. Old Crow sends the team a message to meet with him after the hearing, seeming concerned about the mention of STV and MIG.

They see a petitioner named Montagne up next, who collects statuary and seems to be gradually calcifying himself. He seems to prefer statues, icons, etc of lost cultures, and rambles on about his own lost kingdom, seemingly some 4th World or “Era of the Corpse Lily” collapse, which seems to make the Court uncomfortable. However, he has heard rumors of some sort of planar junction of statues, standing stones, monoliths and the like that he things could allow safer travel of the deep metaplanes if it is discovered and offers a bounty for any who can locate it. Next, Foggy is properly ascended to Queen’s Courtesan, as well as ennobled by Queen’s edict, for services rendered, finalizing her glowup.

Lady Xoc of High Priestess makes a rare appearance at Court, utilizing the Rite of Wishes to publicly claim Sacre Floot as one of her own once again, seemingly offering her protection and a return to the fold, possibly as a counter-move to Hermit in response to Floot’s allegations of torture. Finally, just as the Queen is about to end the Hearing, a strange spirit steps forward and she pauses, which the team hasn’t seen before. The spirits is named Portent and states that although it cannot see it’s own future, it can see dark days ahead for the Court- bargains will be offered, some too good to refuse, and taken them may lead to ruination. With that cryptic warning, Court is adjourned, and the team exits, bumping into Aradia on their way out.

She explains that she’s arranged a meeting with a member of the Magician faction after the team once again seems iffy on allying with Bastard, which is PPJ’s preference. TD and PPJ plan to head to the luncheon and feel Magician out, at least for the night. Creep and Otters head to Bristeluchairt tavern to meet with Old Crow at an open lunch. Otters is briefly distracted by the central feature, a massive chimney with four hearths facing out on each side, over each of which is mounted some massive pelt or skull as well as a unique looking weapon. As Otters stares, she hears the firekeepers at each hearth telling the tale of the hunter who wielded each weapon, and slew each of the great beasts whose remains reside above, as well as suggesting that should anyone hunt a greater beast with a more interesting weapon, they will have their own moment of glory here.

Old Crow sits them down and warns them that the Magical Investors Group is essentially a front for the Black Lodge. Based on the timing of their arrival, he also wouldn’t be surprised if MIG/BL was either partially or fully behind the Shadow faction as well- what better way for a human supremacist group to operate at Court than in secret? Based on intel OC got from some Rogue Lodge contacts, he thinks the BL may have come hear believing that the Court and the Yellowstone Anomaly could be sources of great power, or great danger, as hinted at it in Hansu’s Prophecies 1 and 2. He requests the team dig further, and they agree to. They also see that he still looks injured from Detroit- somewhat depleted and weakened in what may be a permanent manor. He offers payment of a type of the team’s choosing, but hints that the ability to travel the metaplanes is one option.

At the Magician luncheon, TD and PPJ converse with Sven Skarsbard and his lover Chloe Glowworm. They both seem concerned about poison, which eventually leads to the tale of Sven’s poisoning and a past team member assisting them in resolving that, though Sven is still weaker than usual and they’re both paranoid. Chloe seems comfortable making offers and promises on behalf of Magician though, so they do a little interview about what each can off the other. After PPJ rejects her offer to attend their schools and become a Master Magician (partially because he is insulted that they would make him test up from level 1), they get down to business and secure patronage for the night in exchange for the message spell formula that Otters copied at Thisbe’s. Chloe also gives them a list of other optional payments, including a Tarot set, if they wish to secure a longer term arrangement.

They agree to think on it, and exit, meeting up with the rest of the team and all debriefing on what they found out. They go over the Prophecies again, and discover another Tarot set that seems relevant, as well as various worrying hints of what may come. TD and the others also have a few lingering threads they want to follow up on at and around Court, and they once again find themselves without specific dinner plans, so they all agree to spend a moment deciding what to do next. They also discuss if Creep and Thisbe’s child will be part gnome or part chair, if that is the truer representation of Creep’s being now.

The Tale Unfolds
6/17/2024, 10:45:36 AM
Runners
Creep
The Detective
Otters aka Roadkill
Pepper Jack
Heat
Aradia

Aradia and PPJ stay behind to keep an eye on the team’s gear, as PPJ requests not to be left alone (or trusted if seen alone) with news of his dopple/clone/? being inhabited by a powerful Blood Spirit. The rest of the team head to the Trickster’s Buffet, following the pulsing guide sigil on their invitation. They find a door carved with a faded infinity loop and enter, finding themselves in a long hall, into which has been crammed a long sectional table and chairs, with Castle servants squeezing amongst the diners to deliver food. There also seem to be a complicated game of musical chairs going on, where the table nearest the door along with it’s chairs are lifted aloft and sent to the far end, with diners racing after to maintain their places.

This leads to a lot of jostling and opportunistic adjustment of relative placement, in which the team partakes. Creep sneaks along under the table for a while until he realizes that the people who notice him there are amused at his “prank” and the takes a chair just before a disappointed courtier, who is ejected from the dinner, as the team gets the sense that as the feast and table progress, chairs are occasionally removed, so they have to stay on their toes in multiple ways.

Heat and Otters end up paired together, in conversation with a man they quickly ID as Rooster, from descriptions in the team file. He seems exhausted and a little bit edgier than reported and after some clever banter with Heat (Otters proves to be too direct, though she manages to cover for it), they get some intel. Rooster explains that he and an-ex-ex teammate of theirs have been engaged in a long-running series of pranks, and that Test had got him real good by dosing him with Tempo. Thus, he can’t safely sleep often as Shadow spirits will possess his sleeping body if he does, especially given that Shadow spirits are probably seeking broad revenge against the Fae after the destruction of Fauth-Doshgoi.

He offers patronage for the night through Hanged Man, delaying the need for a more permanent commitment for another day, in exchange for two favors- he needs someone to guard him while he sleeps, and he passes them a small sachet of some sort of drug, which he wants them to dose Test with, as Heat had let slip that they knew of Test’s current location and vocation. Figuring that they’ll be back to the Great Library on Kindred at some point, and after Rooster assures them it is a non-lethal prank, they agree.

The table shuffles again, and they find themselves seated near a late middle-aged human woman, who seems to possess some serious spycraft skills. They don’t put it together initially, but from down the table Creep recognizes her as Samantha Roth, the ex-UCAS military former NeoNET spymaster. She explains to Heat and Otters that she got hired on by Johnny Spinrad during NN’s post-Boston collapse, but that after his death, his wife Gabrielle has been sidelining any high-level perceived loyalists, including her. She was sent to the Seelie Court to establish a footprint for Spinrad Global and gain intel.

She compliments the two runners on their strange display at Court the prior morning, which Heat is pleasantly surprised was noticed. She also warns them that conservatives at the Court don’t want outsiders to mess with their “gramarye” aka “grimoire”, which is what the locals call what passes for their network. She explains that Court enforcers known as “Crows” are surely watching the team after their little trick, and that although they aren’t GOD they do have serious matrix skills, and are more attuned to the unique local environment therein.

Otters seems to be losing it a bit from not having hotSIM access for days, and Roth tells her to check out the Geocache room, if they can locate it, as well as to take a visit to the area around Gilik’s tavern, where she might find things more to her liking. As she seems somewhat willing to dispense with local customs of obscure speech and hints at more work available, they ask her to take a walk with them away from the dinner, and she agrees. They quickly outpace the chaotic moving table, and have an informative convo about the state of the matrix at Court.

Roth is pleased to find that the team is already fully briefed on the Null Sect, and that Heat seems to have a personal vendetta against them. She tells him to take a look around when he gets to Geocache and report his findings, as she has suspicions that the Null are attempting an infiltration of the Court from there. She also tells Heat that if he can amass a collection of “pá” coins, he can probably use his rare TM abilities to essentially act as a comms hub for the crew while they are in this strange place, and tells him to further study the coimeadai network to gain an understanding of how this might work.

She offers various payments, including cash if they are as crass as that, but seems cautiously optimistic about having some reliable freelancers on hand. They all split off and head out, disappearing from each other in the dim areas between torches in the endless hallway. Otters and Heat return to the Renraku safehouse, with Otters and Aradia offering to stay behind and wait for the others, while Heat and PPJ bring their still-peacebound crate of illicit gear down to Gilik’s, where Rooster is putting them up for the night. First, Heat uses his TM abilities to check out the Renraku host through a charging port for electronics provided in the bunk room.

Seeing that it is a very high rating hidden host, he decides that the job for Gary Cline/Horizon is currently not possible, so backburners that for now. Meanwhile, at the Everhall dinner, Creep and The Detective have ended up sitting to together, and gradually work their way into conversational range with their host, Garbh Filiochta. She seems interested in them, and the table is amused at Creep’s blatant disregard of conversational and social rules, as this is all seen as a grand jape in this context. Creep is flustered by their amusement, so TD does most of the talking.

They get the feel that Garbh has been tasked with feeling out what the team is up to here, and who, if anyone, they are aligned with. Creep has also spotted 3 masked killers at the dinner, who he assesses are there to protect Garbh rather than the event, and to “deal” with the team if they are a problem. TD manages to initially dissemble successfully, but they realize that they need to give Garbh something to avoid further complications, and so tells her that the team is there working for an Earth client to research an Earth entity using the Court’s excellent libraries, and that they have not permanently aligned with any faction and are delighted to make new connections.

This seems to satisfy for now, though Garbh suggests that an appropriate gift to the Queen for her ongoing tolerance of their presence could be an item to add to her Arcana, or some assistance in helping quash some of the nasty rumors floating around about her. They take the hint, and plan to either leave soon or make an appropriate offering. The table shuffles again, and they find themselves near an ancient courtier named Porl Sandstorm, who seems to know a lot and interestingly though he seems well-connected, is ostentatiously free of any markers that would suggest a Faction affiliation.

He conversationally hints at some of the rumors about the Queen that Garbh had alluded to- apparently in addition to the “artifacts for access” scandal that the team heard hints of at Court the previous morning, there are also longstanding rumors being amplified by the Agents of Clarity that the Queen is herself a doppleganger or some other nefarious entity, and that this should call her entire coronation into question. He phrases this all as if these are awful rumors he has overheard that he ought not repeat but has allowed himself to be convinced to- very smooth.

TD and Creep leave after that, with Garbh telling Creep to attend a certain mansion address if he still seeks a tailor- so it seems like they passed the audition. They leave and manage to catch Heat and PPJ before they head to Gilik’s, so with a slight change of plans, Aradia and the rest of the crew head down there with the gear to get ready for the Feast of Hecate ball later in the evening, while PPJ and Creep head to the mansion wing to seek the address Creep was given. They find it with little trouble, as their corporate temp housing is nearby, currently. And find what looks like various size and style mansion faces jammed in next to each along the sides of a wide corridor, with the Castle seemingly having reshaped itself around them.

They find the address the seek, a small and modest but nice single story house with a charming fence and organized front garden. Approaching the round door, Creep tries the gnome-height knocker at it’s center. PPJ says he will stay outside, as he wants to examine the strange magic here, which suggests that the Castle can shuffle these mansions around as needed in some inscrutable way. Creep enters to find Marcus Ko, the Queen’ tailor, waiting, alongside one of the masked killers from the dinner. Marcus instructs the masked man (Sir Cian) to fetch appropriate tools, and he crouch-walks around the gnome-size house awkwardly to do so.

Creep describes his desires, and Marcus seems interested by the project- making a wearable chair that will allow Creep decent mobility and some armor, while also being able to pose as furniture. He says Creep will need to remain with him for a bit while he readies the garment, and hopes he doesn’t have other plans for the evening. Creep agrees, and in payment offers up his Tarot ticket to Zurich Orbital, which has been weighing on him as he doesn’t really plan to use it, but it is tied to him. Marcus seems happy to accept this, perhaps assuming that as a fellow gnome he can plausibly pass, and Creep sees it transform from a digital token to a paper ticket as he passes it over.

Marcus suggests that this could be an item of great value to him, given how much Foggy gained from her own trip to ZO and the moon. Creep feels relieved to be rid of it and settles in for an evening of measurements and custom tailoring, familiar from his pageant upbringing. The rest of the team leaves Aradia watching over Rooster after having set up some appropriate magical defenses in a back room off of Gilik’s tavern- an impressive 4 story den of assorted debauchery that feels a bit like home to the runners. Otters is also delighted to see some assorted tech here, though it seems like steampunk is the dominant vibe, and she wonders if there are locals or material plane visitors who paradigm-shifted, and assumed it is probably some mix.

There are far more dwarves, as well as gnomes, orks, and other metatypes down here than at Court proper, and they feel they have travelled deep below the Castle to reach this location. They also all had to crawl through a low opening to enter the tavern, seemingly intended to bring everyone down to dwarf height, at least for a moment. With Rooster performatively snoring away, Aradia readies the other 3’s outfits, transforming them magically to white with a hint of silver, as instructed by Niall, after they discuss whether or not to be sartorially disruptive and decide against. They head to the ball, at the Agatarish Dome, and for once have little trouble finding it, feeling almost as if the Castle guides them there.

They find an elegant older Kappa (seemingly the titular Agatarish) hosting, and a stage set up at one end of the large chamber, on which a band is playing- Heat is immediately drawn that direction to study the unusual microtonal modality, and gets what recordings he can before Foggy comes over to converse with them and he reluctantly pulls himself away. Foggy explains the them about the Feast of Hecate and the monthly balls at Court, and Heat goes straight for it and hands over their 6WT Queen of Batons, with a gracious reminder that Niall was behind this. Foggy is delighted and suggests that so long as nothing untoward occurs, this should assure her ascension to the highest rank of courtier, and that if this happens, she will be in an even better position to assist her friends, whoever they may be.
The team agrees that they would love to be friends, and she seems delighted to hear it. She introduces her young cousin Olin ke’Coille, who seems a little small for his formal robe, but has a cute spirit weasel companion and seems to be quite a powerful mage as he levitates off at great speed after politely meeting them. With their task out of the way, they decide to just have a good time, with TD and Otters eventually slipping off as they event descends into full spring orgy mode, after partaking to various degrees. Heat fully commits, grooving to the music and getting it on with an assortment of partners in an assortment of configurations (careful to always keep his cloak on).

The team rests, and decide to all head to Court in the morning, as PPJ and Aradia were able to take shifts watching Rooster, who seems quite relieved to feel well-rested for once. He seems to be collecting tales, as usual, and the team inquires about something else they’ve been curious about- what’s up with Harlequin? Rooster is disappointed that they already know the big reveal that he’s the Jester, but explains that Harly has been mind-wiped and made into a coimeadai- so presumably his memory and self are being securely stored somewhere? Rooster seems to desire the role of Jester for himself so offers his assistance if needed for any future mischief that might help achieve that. This also reminds the team that Harly had split himself intentionally once before, creating Gwynplaine, and wonders what happened to GP as well.

He stays behind to continue eating breakfast at Gilik’s (having ordered the full English) while the team heads back upstairs, following the tolling bells. They enter the Great Hall as the Morning Hearing commences, and TD sees that the tapestries and mosaics today seem to hint at some scandals in the artifact trade at Court- both members of the Queen’s faction trading artifacts for access, as they had heard rumored, and also other high ranking houses of various factions seemingly selling off heirlooms at historical low points to some shadow-y figure. They are unable to figure out more as the crowd roils, but it seems like some scandals are brewing amongst the courtiers in response.

The ignored Shadow Tourmaline is now broadcasting a message in glowing red letters on it’s black surface, saying “Beware the Hidden Master! Are they Traders, or Traitors?” – everyone sees it but carefully looks away. PPJ leaps up to a balcony again, careful to stay in view of the rest of the team. The line of petitioners form. Aneki goes first, and gets approval to expand Renraku’s census into a taxonomical survey of the denizens of the Court and the metaplanes in general, offering recompense for any who participate, and assuring the Court that their studies are purely non-invasive and non-surgical. Heat gets excited as maybe this is a way in to hack their databases for Horizon, though the rest of the team still seems suspicious of Heat’s goals here, though certainly convinceable.

The team is then surprised to see the legendary Ehran the Scribe, who gets approval to set up an employment office, seeking skilled magical practitioners and other specialists for an unspecified project funded by the DIMR, offering competitive rates. He instructs any interested parties to seek him out, and the team also notices the conspicuous absence of Frosty Jane, who usually accompanies him on these sort of tasks. Next a plucky young reporter named Suzuki Jones, who the team knows of from the file, and Heat personally remembers from internal Horizon biz, gets up and requests permission to conduct interview with any prospective attendees of the forthcoming Grand Tour seasons.

It seems that these Euro-focused mobile gatherings of the rich and famous are starting to include more and more Fae due to the planes’ relative proximity, and Jones gets temporary approval only for on the record interviews- no gossip or surveillance. As Heat knows she often strings for the London-based (and Horizon-funded) conspiracy rag “The All-Seeing Eye”, he thinks these are reasonable restrictions to place. As the scandals brew about the tapestries and mosaics of the day, the team overhears some rumor chatter, that the Queen may be a secret member of the Vigilance faction, and discover some of the sources of the gossip about the Queen being a dopple- she often has conversations with some undetectable entity, and also some of the rumors seem to actually have been hinting towards Aina Dupree, the Ebon Queen/Eiolai of the Unseelie.

Finally, Chambray Cliff rises to speak again, and goes on a long speech about the dangers of the Deep Metaplanes and travellers from those realms, as well as the threat, both recent and ongoing, of Blood spirit impersonators at Court (perhaps playing off the heightened dopple fears). He then turns to point to PPJ specifically, and calls for the Rite of Testimony to be used on him and some other suspicious travellers from throughout the Court, who have been seen to receive messages from the Shadow Tourmaline. The Queen agrees, and PPJ and some other courtiers are politely seized by magic and Court guards, who start to escort them to the dungeons, as the Rite will be carried out at the next day’s Morning Hearing.

TD, fearing for PPJ if he is left alone, accompanies him voluntarily, insisting that they also be arrested, much to Cliff’s confusion. They are escorted to the dungeon, and put in adjacent, slightly too small cells, tilted out towards an open end that falls into some sort of void. They can converse, barely, by shouting into the hole in the floor provided for expelling waste, though with the audio distortion, TD is unable to fully confirm that this is the “real” PPJ talking back, though he knows the right stuff. They also hear some hideous laughter echoing from another adjacent cell, and TD briefly laughs along before almost getting subsumed and losing themselves in the laughter. They manage to pull back and decide not to engage further for now, trying to remember anything they had read of the Castle dungeons in the team file.

The rest of the crew, a bit shocked at the turn of events, decides to proceed with their day’s plans, and Aradia sets up back to back meeting with Niall and Nadja Daviar. They converse quickly with Niall after the Heating rapidly concludes, and he provides the two Tarot cards as promised. He also reminds them that if they free TD, or wish themselves to, he would be interested in discussing further what they saw in the Pendulum Room a few nights previous. Aradia and Creep head to lunch with Daviar in the Cube dining hall again, while Heat and Otters split off and head to the Geocache.

Aradia and Creep pass the cards over, which Nadja is delighted by, complimenting them on their speed and efficiency, compared to many in this frustrating place. She offers intel in response as promised and fills in the team on the holes in their understanding of GW and Zebulon’s pasts- Yuichotol’s death in the 4th World at the hands of the Therans using blood magic (AZT precursors, hence that beef), GW reconstituting her “soul” into a fettered great form spirit called Ghost Scale, and GW falling out with Dunkelzahn due to the dark magics he used to do this. Ghost Scales then herself being disrupted by Lofwyr during the early days of the Scourge, explaining that beef as well. The Great Tree that was all that remained of All-Wings burned around this time as well, leaving only the Ashes that GW seeks.

Nadja goes on to explain how the ritual that GW used to create Ghost Scales was similar enough to a locality binding to allow her fragments to coalesce around a place- in this case Denver, during the early Gold Rush, and become something of a Spirit of the City, though weak and without real power during the magical downtime. She became further fractured during the Great Ghost Dance due to the fragmentation of the USA into the UCAS and NAN, and then more so as Denver itself was chopped into slices as the Treaty City. GW’s reemergence from his astral kaer when Big D sacrificed himself to stave off the Horrors allowed GW to establish himself in Denver and begin working to reassemble, Ghost Scales, now called Zebulon, again.

He did what he could to amass power but needed more, and used the ritual conducted by Aina Dupree (the Ebon Queen) and others to close the Watergate Rift (to halt the entrance of Shedim to Earth) to do so. Aina was believed killed in the ensuing magical explosion, and her body sucked into the rift as GW also entered just before it’s closing. By tying the local hyperplanes artificially together, he was able to stabilize Zebulon, but at the expense of the city, which began suffering from the Reality Rips of a few years back. He reemerged and further established himself as the lord of Denver, with Zebulon assisting him.

This is where his beef with Harlequin emerges- Harly was Aina’s on/off lover (both are thought to be amongst the Immortal Elves), and believing her killed, he decided to get revenge on GW, executing his plane to sever Denver’s hyperplanes yet again, undoing GW’s work and fragmenting Zebulon once more. According to Nadja, GW is still willing to sacrifice the people of Denver to save what he can of her, and has been offering spirit sanctuary to any spirits who swear fealty and make their way to the Mile High Realm, seemingly aiming to bolster his strength there. She also posits that GW and his allies must somehow be benefiting from the magical exchange between Earth and the MHR that contributes to the ongoing rifts.

With all of this laid out, the team feels like they have satisfactorily completed that job, and Creep asks one final question- with what was down to Harly after losing his duel to Alachia, what happened to Gwynplaine? Nadja chuckles and says that they certainly weren’t rejoined beforehand, but that GP also doesn’t walk free, suggesting that the team could do worse than to check the Castle’s dungeons for the resident mad clown, if they wish to intervene. With that all taken care of, she reminds them to look into Rex Coll when they get a chance, and they depart lunch and head back to Gilik’s.

Meanwhile, after some wandering, Heat and Otters find the Geocache, and see a physical prop representation of a laser grid slide open as they enter. They quickly notice that one of the Crows they were warned of is watching them, but both are delighted to find an actual wireless matrix they can dive into, which weirdly also has a physical manifestation, in that they enter a green line grid on black background kind of classic 2050’s matrix. Looking around, they spot some wireless repeaters seemingly installed by NeoNET before their collapse, and Heat also notices that the local “noosphere” seems to be generating Resonance energy, which NN look to have cleverly used to power their tech, creating a strange infinite loop that seem to be slowly expanding the reach of it’s effect field.

They also see 8 large floating shapes, which all seem to represent the 8 AAA members of the Corp Court from the 2050’s. Seeing that they are all fairly high security, Heat takes a closer look at the NN node, the weakest among them, and sees it is crawling with Null vines, which have begun to emerge. Otters goes to distract the Crow, pointing out their gardening problem, though the Crow seems to take this literally and suggest she speak to Banleigh Swale, the Castle groundskeeper. This allows Heat to quickly try to enter the NN host- he rejects an offered MARK and hacks his way in, quickly exiting again as he sees it is fully gutted on the inside and infested with Null icons.

Putting together that this is the node that Test had hacked and used for data dives and comms out on his last visit, Heat puts together that be utilizing the Null Node of that Foundation so extensively, Test seems to have opened a pathway for the Null to enter here, and seeing as the chamber seems to operate on an expanding loop, likely have the potential to expand their presence. Lacking any options on how to prevent this at the moment, and with the Crow remaining obstinate, they had back to Gilik’s for now to meet up and debrief. Heat wants to interfere in the Harlequin biz, and suggests they at least contact Aina/The Ebon Queen to let her know of Harly’s present circumstances, and see what happens from there. He also wants to follow up with Samantha Roth and see if there is a way to halt the Null’s progress here.

For now, they need a patron for the night and have no current dinner plans, and 1/3rd of their party is in jail, so they have some thinking to do about next steps….

Night Time is the Right Time
6/10/2024, 8:09:45 PM
Runners
Creep
The Detective
Otters aka Roadkill
Pepper Jack
Heat

After dinner, the team discusses the Tarot set that Nadja Daviar is looking to collect and decides it likely has to do with Ghostwalker and portals- so, relevant. The team briefly discusses the potential dangers and then decides to all go wander the castle, accept Creep, who goes to bed in their Renraku-provided corp bunks. PPJ tries to manifest his way to one of the sought Tarot cards, but quickly gets lost amidst some back halls, where some strange mushrooms seem to be in use as some form of air filtration. He eventually finds his way back to their room, seeing some castle servants feeding some wall-mounted caged glow-worms that light up, lamp-like. He also goes to sleep, a bit discouraged.

Aradia and The Detective end up sticking together, and searching for a fun mystery, and find their way to the Pendulum Room, where they join a strange masked dance, though they cannot hear the music. Aradia loses the beat first, and is thrown into a black void over the edge of the swinging platform room they were dancing on. TD quickly follows suit, though they were first able to spot and memorize a strange note, that they found themselves able to read even while aware that it was not written in a language they knew.

Both in similar but separate voids, they each spot a door in the far distance, as well as both seemingly one peaceful and one exciting option to each side. Both get the sense that something may be following them, and Aradia levitates herself over to the door and enters. TD, lacking magic, takes a little longer to reach the door, and is also more curious, waiting until the thing following (some kind of massive, camouflaged, rotting centipede) gets upsettingly close, and then also exits.

They find themselves stepping out of seemingly random doors elsewhere in the Castle, and Aradia manages to make her way back to TD, and as they see the sun is starting to come up (how long were they dancing and in that void?) they head back to the room to try to get a little rest. Otters, meanwhile, has sought out a library, looking for some more info on artifacts and the like, and finds some more info on Allwings and the Ashes of the Great Tree, as well as some references to other texts to seek in another library. Hearing some noises throughout the pitch black library, she decides not to push it, and also returns to the room just as the sun is coming up.

Heat, seeking a musical excursion, finds his way out a narrow stair to the woods just outside the Castle, where he finds some Fae dancing in a perfect moonbeam in clearing, inside of an odd ring of mushrooms. He quietly records their songs and chants, though he can’t quite figure the tuning, tone or pattern as it seems to be some sort of microtonal round. However, he jumps into the circle, sampling his recordings to the beat, and seemingly accepted as a participant. He awakens late the next morning, with a bad moonburn, feeling thoroughly sore and danced out, but with the strange recordings intact.

The rest of the team, minus Otters who has decided to sleep in, make their way to the Morning Hearing in the Great Hall. PPJ carefully focuses, wanting to get to one of the balconies, as they aren’t quite full, and manages to leap up to one, seemingly calling on some approval from somewhere in his mind to gain the final push. He takes a spot, leaning on the tree branch balustrade, and sees someone who was sedately levitating towards that spot redirect. Creep, seeking a tailor to get an appropriate local fit, spots two promising candidates: a courtier with a foppish style who seems well-dressed, and a small gnome man who seems to intentionally obscure himself within the Queen’s Retinue.

Creep tries to make eyes and gestures at the Queen’s tailor, without being too obvious or causing an issue, and isn’t sure which if either of those he succeeded in, but definitely did something. He then begins ignoring the Hearing and focusing on the tapestries and mosaics, which seem to be shifting to tell a new tale- seemingly the tail of 6Cyl’s love letters to Zeha Cadgris being read aloud in Court, much to her embarrassment. Comparing notes with others on the crew, they confirm that it looks like Bastard and Hanged Man are showing as having revelled in embarrassing the torturer, while Hermit seems to have somehow politically benefited in a way the team doesn’t understand.

Heat finally stumbles in, and is shocked and delighted to see Horizon CEO Gary Cline is one of the early petitioners, and arranges a lunchtime coffee via the message system. PPJ gets a mysterious pitch black missive with a cryptic warning, seemingly one of the the notes the Tourmaline Obelisk has been sending out since everyone was instructed to ignore it. He stealthily reads and pockets it. TD composes a poetic response to what they experienced the previous night, and holds it aloft hoping to send it to a relevant party. It snaps out of their hand, and travels to Niall O’Connor, who reads it and makes interested eye contact, though it is unclear if he summoned it to hand.

Aradia also gets an invite (with guests) the evening’s Ball, The Feast of Hecate, from Foggy, who lightly berates Aradia for not contacting her the previous day. They see Cline get approval for the quarterly review of Horizon’s “New Horizons” astral travel agency to continue operations, after a dense political interchange where different factional reps come out for and against it, with some hints that there is some scandalous “artifact for access” trade going on behind the scenes. Possibly to contradict this, or perhaps missing the nuance, Cline passes a “small token” of a gift to the Queen, via Foggy.

A mortal man named Greg Bright petitions for more materials for his ongoing maze outside the Castle walls, and the team catches some gossip that he stumbled into the Court through a maze a long while ago, but has lately been taken on as “the Queen’s mazemaker” as a bit of a lark. His request is approved. A few other petitioners come and go, and the Hearing’s disperse. Otters awakens to find a note, from the former Jester Garbh Filiochta, inviting the team to a Trickster’s Buffet that evening. After meeting up and confirming that the dinner won’t conflict with the Ball invite from Foggy, they plan to do both, and then split for lunch and midday investigations.

Otters and Creep head for a history library, eventually tracking it down, and begin digging further into GW/Allwings/etc. They find a bunch of intriguing 4th and 2nd World history, seemingly often having been compiled by scribes of a lost kingdom called “Barsaive”, and put together more of the story. They also find further speculation about the origins of magic, Resonance, immortals, what happened to dragons during the Downtime, as well as some hints to the Court having it’s own long, dark and traumatic history that seems to mirror the material plane’s to some degree. Of most note is that they seem to have uncovered the origins of GW’s beef with AZT, an explicit part of their job. They also discover that a dragon known as Moleskin, who is holed up under the Vatican, has been instrumental in pushing monotheism throughout recent history, with a stated goal of reuniting the “Prime Passion.”

When Heat hears about this, he decides that god is real, and maybe he will start worshipping something. The rest of the team doesn’t quite get it. Meanwhile, TD and PPJ have teamed up and headed out to seek Tarot, hoping to advance their job with Nadja, and quickly run into Niall, who seems to have been waiting for them. They trade some info, and he agrees to help them achieve some of the lower number cards they seek, as he hints that he has recently acquired some cards that he has long sought due to acquiring some of the cards the team previously lost on the plane of Carcean. He gives them some tips to a card from the Queen’s Arcana that Foggy “misplaced”, and which may be the last barrier to her full ascension given her recent success on the Moon.

Realizing that they have the 6WT equivalent card in their possession, Aradia proposes just passing that along, and they decide to run that by the team. They can always seek out the missing card, as Niall believes he knows where it is, and seems to think there will be some sort of trap or ambush, which may be why Foggy has avoided reacquiring it herself. Niall suggests they should talk further of what they witnessed in the Pendulum room also, once they’ve completed this first task. All he asks for now is that they let Foggy know that he has been her benefactor in this matter. They agree.

Heat and and Aradia have coffee with Gary Cline, and Heat is surprised/impressed to find that he has an exact equivalent fancy Turkish drip setup to the one in his LA office. Cline gives them the usual bluff, hale and hearty treatment, and seems very charming and friendly (but also pressed for time). He sets them a few tasks on the metaplanes if they’d like Horizon’s assistance further there, or if they just want to do some work, basically corporate espionage against Renraku and AZT. He provides some details, seemingly under the assumption that they’ll take the gigs, and then heads out, telling them to check in with other Horizon reps here as needed.

Aradia is a bit amused by him overall, and Heat as usual wants to get paid. The team starts figuring out plans for the evening, with some dress tips from Niall to try not to make asses of themselves. They also review the team file and compare notes to try to keep piecing everything together in the dense sea of proper nouns they have found themselves floating in.

All Wings All The Time

The team reviews their recent betrayal, as well as the new info they’ve learned about “The Guide”. With that dealt with, they say their goodbye’s to Aneki for now, who gives them directions to the temporary corp housing they’ll use for the night, and they make their way over to the Exile’s Return, where they meet up with the exhausted Creep, get a quick debrief, and enter to find that Daviar has saved them some seats at one of the large banquet tables.

As advertised, this feast seems to be comprised of travellers, outsiders, and people who have been away from Court for a while, but the food is still excellent, even at this least prestigious of events. They converse with Daviar, and though they aren’t able to confirm it is for sure the “real” her she seem to at least know the right stuff and not be a full-on imposter. She also seems suspicious of Rex Coll (both her and Glimmerscale have become quite curious as to what is happening to all the live capture bounty subjects that the DF has paid out for over the years, which seems to have been Coll’s area) and wants more intel, hinting that her exit from her board position wasn’t entirely voluntary, but that she hopes to reveal her adversaries weaknesses and plans by allowing them a position of power, for now.

She also says she is seeking four specific Tarot cards due to imagery she believes is important, and says that is why she came to the Court. She says that if the team can provide her with one or more of them, she will trade in the info that they seek, or in assistance finding anything that she doesn’t know herself, and they agree. As a show of good faith, she shares that they can find more about the Ashes of the Great Tree in Dunkelzahn’s Will, another topic to research. With some good leads and a successful first day at Court, the team calls it a night.
