He quickly summons up a ward around their booth for privacy, and seems delighted that they brought their own white noise machine.

He seems very droll and upper crust British, and manages to slyly make a fool of a number of team members in a way that seems meant to be fun, including the others in it. He does seem quite interested in the glyphs, and also confirms that he is an astral travel expert and can help them prep for and begin their journey to Zecorporatum and Carcean, though he warns them of the dangers of the journey and they don’t mention their goals or Grey Cell’s involvement.

He also confirms he was paying rent on Kara Jay’s old shop, The Phoenix, which burnt down shortly after Heat coaxed her out of there. When they explain their theories about her e-ghost and her survival, he seems relieved, and they get a feeling he was a true friend of hers back in the day. He looks very young and smooth-skinned to be that old, but looks human, so maybe just has a good skincare routine. In exchange for all this assistance, he requests they play a prank on Cat and steal a large statue from her office. They are a little leery, but believe him when he says it is long-running gag between the two.

He gives them some intel and specifics on the statue (a 400kilo dragon) but says they will have to do the rest, and to bring it to his shop, Mile High ArcanoArcheaology, when they are done. He has a few parameters- no casualties, no major property damage, don’t get caught, and they agree. TD starts keeping an eye on Cat, who is running the bar and chatting to various people, and steps in to distract her, while providing occasional tips and encouragement on comms. Heat does a quick survey of the security system, and gets in, turning off cameras and forcing the elevator open before having to flee before IC, after wiping his presence with TM skills. Helpfully, this further distracts Cat as she gets some “intruder detected” alerts.

PPJ and Aradia make Aradia, Creep and Otters invisible in the bathrooms, and all of them except PPJ sneak out and aboard the elevator while PPJ maintains the spells. Otters orders 400kilo of weights and flies the VTOL over to pick them up, and then sneaks it into the Hub and has it hovering outside Cat’s office window. PPJ does some astral projection and manifesting to scout ahead, and spots a series of traps which he warns them of.

Otters and Creep manage to tagteam cracking open and bypassing the biometrics lock on the elevator and get it to ascend, and then take out a window to allow the drone in. Aradia uses her new “Ward Pusher” ability to slide them all in past the alarm ward set around the stature, and Creep and Otters manage to disable the first trap- a pressure plate. Downstairs, Heat joins TD in distracting Cat. Creep and Otters rig up a jumper cable to bypass the electrical mesh around the statue, and cut their way through once they’ve redirected the power.

Then Aradia casts two levitate spells at once, lifting the statue with one, while pushing the spring loaded pin under it down with the other spell. Creep carefully moves weight plates from the drone to on top of the pin as Aradia eases off that spell, and loads the statue in the now empty drone. Having assensed a small magical charge in the statue, Aradia manages to push all of them back out of the ward again, and Otters flies the drone back out the window as Aradia levitates her and Otters down to the street.

Creep puts the window back, and cleans up, leaving as little trace of their presence as possible (aside from a very crude sketch of a dragon he left on one of the weights), and then manages to override the elevator again, ride it back down, and sneak back to the bathroom, before emerging as if he just needed some time in there to empty his leg. The team exits the bar, and heads over to Arcane’s spot, where they are greeted by what at first looks like a cat but turns out to be a bundle of vines and thorns in that shape, and tells them to head upstairs as Arcane is waiting.

They head up a fire escape to the second level entrance door over the commercial space, and knock. They are greeted by a large animated suit of armor, which says “I’ll take that” and unloads the statue from the drone with little trouble, impressive given it’s weight. The armor waves them through to a library/office where they find Arcane waiting. Impressed with themselves, they can’t help but describe how clever they were in doing the job, which he somewhat condescendingly plays along with.

They get down to biz, and he explains that he has some of Kara Jay;s old journals, and hoped to decipher the code of the glyphs with them, but although he has made some progress they seem only semi-sensical. They are related to attracting spirits, but he’s not sure what type or why for, and doesn’t know why they were being put up as AR icons, since few spirits would be able to interact with those. So, still confusing but some progress. They share a bit more info about The Seer’s current state and location, and he agrees to send recordings of him reading from her journal, through StepMAMA to her mysterious caretakers, to hopefully help in her recovery.

With that settled, Creep starts dropping hints about Cornerstones and Tarot as possible payments for astral help, as Arcane doesn’t seem very interested in money. Arcane tells them some tips and tricks for safe® astral travel, and suggests a few items to acquire- the modified Shade and Shadow Cloaks he’s able to provide the specs for, and Aradia is able to order them through her AZT contact, Simonetta Chandra, who once again threateningly flirts about “needing to hear all about what these are for”, though Aradia successfully demurs for now.

The team doesn’t quite have the funds to get all the cloaks made and bonded, so they tell Arcane they’ll accept his offer of help with actually getting to ZCorp, but probably in around a week’s time- he seems grateful for their helping Kara Jay. He also tips them to some other newer/rarer tech to assist with astral travel- a “Map of the Astral Ways” and a “Sextant of Worlds” copy, which is both true and linked can greatly assist with arriving in the right destinations, as the planes tend to move around a lot relative to each other.

He says he can likely secure them some of these in exchange for a Cornerstone piece and/or Major Arcana from the Tarot, though he hints that there may be multiple Tarot decks and only some planes even really care about them at all. They say they’ll think about it, but ask him to make some soft enquiries. They head back to the safehouse for now, and with Darien Black giving basically the same glyph intel, report into the Guide. He seems satisfied, and says “job done” for now, and he’ll be in touch when has more work. He also authorizes sharing the full glyph set with each researcher in case they can learn anything further.

The team spend a while looking at Tarot and debating how to proceed. With Fargus Sol’s assistance after Creep calls him, they make some progress on understanding how to use and the potential of the Aes Sidhe Banrigh card, but decide to hold off for now, hoping that it can either be an emergency astral traveller or potentially a resurrection, when needed, depending on which interpretation proves correct. They do decide to make the Cornerstone piece that Creep spotted amidst their set, and debate for a while who to sell to.

With Aradia’s review of the team file, they decide to go with Renraku, hoping to further the team’s rep with Kami Aneki if they ever find themselves in the Seelie Court. They use another CEO call to set up the sale, and Heat is impressed by the fact that the CEO is a good negotiator, much to everyone else’s amusement. He does agree to the terms, though he seems to know some about the Cornerstone and reports that they are offering “a lesser piece”. They set up a meeting at a mall in the Hub, in a coffee shop, figuring high security and in public is probably good.

They get there first, and lay claim to one of the bathrooms. Renraku shows up in a stealthed VTOL shortly after, broadcasting an AR landing zone, and coming in fast. Blue Samurai deploy with an executive, and they pay all the civilians to exit the coffee shop, including the staff. They watch, bemused, as Creep fuddles around in his leg compartment to get the two cards while trying not to show that they have a bunch, and then combines them to make the Cornerstone.

As before, it appears in a sort of negative pressure pop in the air, and the Renraku exec carefully picks it up in gloved hands, and puts it in a biofiber case that one of the Blue Samurai unfold for him. He nods to them, and they kick another biofiber bag over to the team, and exit, not waiting to see if the team has anything further to say, boarding and launching the VTOL before going full stealth again. Impressed and a little scared, the team checks their haul, and finds it all there- the Sextant and the Map, though it seems like the map has plenty of space for upgrades so they probably got a slightly lower value one.

They do both seem magical, which seems promising, though they won’t be able to fully confirm their quality until they are on the metaplanes. Satisfied for now, they head back to the safehouse again, not giving too much thought to if any civilians or other interested parties might be curious about the exchange that just took place. Cat calls, asking if they are up for some work, and since she doesn’t seem pissed about the dragon and some of them really need money to get ready for the astral jaunt, they agree. She sends them to an encrypted digital meeting space, where they find an avatar of their Johnson from a few jobs back, Stiletto.

She explains that she was able to pull some data off the fried cyberdeck they retrieved from the ARES spider, and she has a new POI she needs them to look into. Based on her intel, this POI is only known as “The Auditor”, and is a completely hairless, extremely slender, middle-aged human. He will be meeting with Evo’s regional VP in two days at a fancy restaurant in the Hub, and Stiletto wants the team to surveil the meeting, She explains that The Auditor has already met with various other corp execs but that they missed those meetings so they need to get this one documented.

She gives some details about the restaurant, as well as some job parameters- basically, no collateral damage, don’t get caught, don’t blow her investigation. They agree after she offers another large payday, and start looking into it. Based on the intel provided, they have a few ideas to mind of how they might bypass security to get a recording of the meet, and Otters uses her new high lifestyle SIN to get reservations at the restaurant (though not the exclusive VIP roof level where the meeting will take place) for that evening, planning to bring along 3 team-mates as her guests to do an initial recon pass.

Whistling While We Work

Snow White, a wealthy AGS corp princess who moonlights as a shadowrunner, has accumulated a following of 7 devoted dwarves. Luckily, she has cultivated her crew well, and given their varied specialties, the team has gained some regional renown for capably pulling off some high level jobs. Though many are a little creeped out by the cult-like structure of the organization, they work steadily and have all been further enhancing their skills and gear with some recent paydays.

They get a call from one of their usual fixers to set up a meet with a new “Herr Johnson”, Mr. K. They head their in a chauffered limo, and SW does all the important talking as per usual. Mr. K breaks it down- this is a high risk, high reward job involving some complicated travel, but with some major benefits to metahumanity if they succeed. Are they in? After SW accepts, you bet they are. They negotiate for a major payday with half upfront, and go shopping to gear up.

They’ve only been told the first stage of the job for now- a military style transport plane will take them to Lagos, where they will do a quick job for some local pro-social smugglers to get safely moved across some borders into Asamando. They are given a contact number for a fixer there for the next stage. With some new toys and specialty gear and ammo purchased, they board the plane and head out, with SW telling her “daddy dearest” that she’ll be away for a while, making sure to call him at work to avoid her annoying stepmom.

They land in a cleared jungle area on the north side of the lagoon in Lagos, and Sneezy immediately has a bad allergic reaction to all the new local wildlife, so Dopey carries him in the sling he usually uses to haul around an astral-projecting Sleepy. They load themselves and their gear into a military style transport truck, and head into town to the changeling district of Ilasa Maja, to a brothel called the 7th Hand. There they meet a local fixer named Obi Akinlaja, who says he will act as a go-between for the team and the Daughters of Yemaja, the local group they need to impress to get in good with some safe smugglers, who wont sell them for food to the ghouls in Asamando.

They agree to the job- rescue some captives from a flesh peddling syndicated called the Babakku’s safehouse near the Apapa port. They are told to use the truck for the job, and bring the rescued captives to a certain location in the port, where they will meet the smugglers for the next leg of the journey. Grumpy takes over driving and they head to the Babakku safehouse. Sneezy quickly pwns their basic network and gets cam feeds and internal guard comms. There are 12 captives, all in one area, and 6 guards scattered throughout the rest of the safehouse, which is composed of 4 shipping containers crudely cut and welded together into a cube.

Dopey takes up surveillance for any backup arriving, while SW gets the rest organized. Bashful goes invisible and approaches the door, while Sleepy projects into the captive area, manifest, and tells them to stay down as help is on they way. Doc launches some drones for recon and surveillance, and Happy watches the front with spells ready. Sneezy hacks a guards gun inside to shoot the flashbang trap rigged to the door, and Bashful kicks it in, charging inside and smashing two guards with his dual axes.

Doc, Happy, and Snow White (via her manservant drone) take the opportunity to open fire on the surprised guards inside, quickly taking half of them done, while Dopey takes an assault cannon shot straight through the wall at another one inside that Sneezy has tagged on their TacNET. Doc spots a truck of reinforcements incoming and Dopey blows out the engine block from around a corner with some drone assisted targeting. They make quick work of the remaining guards as Grumpy plants some explosive charges outside, and manages to carefully blow the welds and drop the prisoner container straight into their truck. The only complication is that Bashful gets covered in ghoul blood while axeing the final guard, who seems to be the leader. He does manages to snag his commlink though.

Grumpy, having pre-mapped multiple routes and with further drone assistance, gets them to the port with no trouble, where they meet Obi again, but have to move to a second location after he realizes they forgot to tag erase the captives. Having done so, they leave the truck with Obi’s bodyguard, a troll named Abiola Fashola, and Obi makes an intro for them to the Fanti family of smugglers who will take them around the coast and up the Mouhoun river into Asamando. He tells them to carefully obey the rules aboard the Fanti boats and they’ll be fine. Sneezy does some quick hacking on the commlink they got from the ghoul guard and finds that he was a go-between for the Babakku and a ghoul fixer named “The Butcher” who seems to have some sort of special instructions for what kind of captives he wants.

There is a little drama amongst the dwarves as they have to split up and all want to remain with SW (aside from Grumpy, who is performatively intransigent as usual). SW gives a stirring speech with a little song to get them all to behave, and they head out. They manage to behave themselves, and few of them even enjoy their time at sea, helping out on the small craft, which seem to be a true family biz, with people of all ages aboard and involved. The only issue is Bashful’s refusal to speak to anyone outside the group and frequent habit of turning invisible and standing around in the way. He also seems to be coming down with something, maybe from the ghoul blood he got in his mouth and elsewhere?

They have to shuffle boats a few times to take turns staying near SW, but otherwise manage to bribe their way upriver with no trouble, avoiding Asamandan patrols and getting dropped off in a small grove alongside the river. They are told to call Dancer via secure satlink only, and stay hidden in the grove until picked up. The Fanti bid their farewells, and make quick time back downriver. SW calls in as promised, but initially fails some sort of test Dancer is using to confirm ID. Calling back, she smooths things over, and he tells them to lay low til morning, as Infected activity reduces in daylight, so they’ll smuggle them overland to Nyamkopon then.

They set a watch and try to get some rest. Sleepy ends up having to cavort with some interested spirits to keep them from getting upset, but otherwise the night passes uneventfully, with only distant border patrols spotted. In the morning, Dancer shows up with two rugged-ized bush trucks, and lets Grumpy drive one as they load up and head to the city. Even with their intel and a little research the team is surprised to find such a full-on new-built metropolis here, as well as the widespread, free public matrix throughout the country.

SK’s mining investment is apparently paying dividends! As is DBO’s, and the other various interested corps. Dancer takes them to straight into town, and warns them that if they are stopped they will need to fight their way out, as there’s no good way to explain their presence and armaments in the current political situation. Luckily, they avoid entanglements, partially due to Grumpy’s pre-planning of routes and skilled countersurveillance driving.

They arrive at what looks like a reproduction of an English country manor, and pull into the large carriage house out back. After getting progressively more worried about Bashful’s condition, Doc has kept an eye on it but can’t do much, and Sleep has confirmed the presence of HMHVV in Bashful’s aura. Dancer brings them straight to the basement, where he introduces them to the family doctor, Baxton Gresh, who says he can insure a peaceful death for Bashful or try to ensure a successful transition to sapience, but Infected. He explains that he, and the Helling family above, were all Infected and forced to flee London due to the stigma, but that while they are grateful for Asamando’s hospitality, they are working with and funding those who believe the nation could be a better version of itself without, say, the metahuman trafficking.

After confirming that SW can make use of his new powers, Bashful says he wants to be a sapient Infected, and Gresh takes him down to the surgical suite, suggesting the others watch remotely to avoid Infection risks themselves. After some hours, he reports it was a success, so they rest a little easier and continue their research into how to complete the next leg of the job, with Dancer briefing them on the details. There is some sort of ritual being done, somewhere in town, by the Ordo Maximus. They are smuggling in captives, especially ones with high essence, and moving them about secretly. And due to a previous team’s missions, they know Taco Temple is involved.

SW calls her “daddy dearest” again, and gets an old, rejected SK proposal for a subarcology in the downtown Nyamkopon area, directly below where the Taco Temple flagship store and offices sit. He’s not sure why they bid it there, since TT has “always been there”, but it was going to make use of some convenient nearby old mining tunnels to turn them into underground transit paths and connective walkways. The team also knows from the Lagos job that The Butcher is a likely source of more intel about the metahuman trafficking.

They decide to first investigate the TT flagship, and head in, ordering a bunch of food, even though Dancer had warned them of strange ingredients. Interestingly, Sleepy is unable to spot anything off about the food here. SW is also able to slyly order off the “secret menu” and get a bonedust shaker for Bashful to get some nutrients as a gnawer. Sneezy does some matrix recon, and spots that there is a hidden nested host within the main host, which seems to be doing something connected via hardline only to something underground, running down a central shaft that the restaurant/HQ is built around.

With no clear way in, but evidence of underground activity, they head into the extensive underground transit system and start poking around referencing the old SK schematics. With some good skills at such things, they find a hidden door showing signs of recent use in an otherwise disused service area, and manage to pop it open without tripping anything. Descending into dark stairs, they all flip on their thermographic flashlights, aside from Doc who uses his eyelights, and SW who relies on her goggles.

With Grumpy skillfully guiding them with Happy’s assistance, they find another hidden door, this one with a chemseal. Not knowing what they’ll find inside, Happy distributes all the usual mission drugs and gets everyone dosed up, as well as passing around some Guts. They use some B&E tools and manage to get in while Sneezy loops the cam feeds, which all seem hardwired, and Sleepy gets them past the alarm ward just inside. Passing through another similar door, they enter what seems to be a medical facility- could this be the hidden Ordo Maximus clinic they’ve heard is rumored to exist somewhere under Downtown?

Doc sends out the drones on patrol since there isn’t any local matrix activity. Spotting some guards, he tags them and the team sets an ambush, with Sneezy jamming their comms as the rest of the team takes them out. They manage to keep one alive long enough for Sleepy to Mind Probe. and find out that there is a large freight elevator on the far side of the clinic that only goes down, that captives are frequently brought through and down, and that they were told not to ask questions, and in the event of a raid to try only to protect clinics and allow intruders to descend.

Not wanting to but having no other choice, they descend the elevator, and instantly get into a firefight with some Ordo Myrmidons, who make short work of many of the team’s drones, as well as Happy, with their monofil whips. Sleepy and Sneezy are both not having much luck effecting them given their Dissonant and Anti-Magic effect fields, so Sleepy projects ahead, while Sneezy dashes around the combat to avoid it and get deeper in. A vamp mage in ritual robes also emerges and heads up to see what’s going on, they manage to take him out pretty quickly, but not before he can call for “Martin” to come deal with the unwanted guests. Grumpy places charges on the elevator, just in case.

SW finishes the mage off with Sleepy’s cleverly purchased wood pulp rounds, which seems to halt his regen ability, just as a massively powered up Martin deVries charges in and starts attacking them. Grumpy sneaks past the fight to see what’s happening further in, having heard some chanting, and Doc sends the roto drone along with. Dopey and Bashful try their best to hold DeVries at bay and protect SW, but Bashful, having feasted some on the ghoul guards they killed in the clinic as well as the Myrmidons here, is starting to get mentally overwhelmed by some sort of mental summons to come deeper as well.

Down below, Sneezy spots a massive data terminal with a map of the globe, seemingly showing a distribution of TT locations and frequent consumers, and he darts past the two vamp techs working the terminal to touch it and fry it, using the final service of the massive Sprite he compiled for the job. Grumpy watches in horror as the vamp techs tear into his prone body, but is busy planting charges around a ritual circle protected by a magic barrier, having spotted it flickering and correctly assumed that Sleepy is assaulting it in astral space.

And Doc has spotted two other horrifying things- there is a pen full of captives seemingly all being slowly drained of blood by the ritual, which is being assisted by a massive bound blood spirit, and there is a floating chunk of reflective black stone, to which a slim vampire dressed in purple rags is bound. They seem to have various healing wounds on them but no obvious source of injury. Heat opens it and is told to call a number to “collaborate against a threat”. He calls, and is horrified to find that he has made contact with GOD, or someone posing as such. They essentially force Heat into agreeing to look further into Ex Machina and Leanna Octave, which the team kinda wanted to do anyway, and tell them that the funds available were seized and transferred from the deceased CD’s, and that Heat is welcome to tell his team whatever he likes as to where it came from.

Creep and Otters swap some implants and get some new stuff, and Heat starts prepping to upgrade his legs to he can move a bit better, after filling the team in on their new assignment from GOD, having been also told they’ll be keeping a close eye on him. Doubek also gives them some initial intel on Octave and her activities in town, though she seems likely to have gone into hiding after losing the CD’s as allies. The team next heads to the Paradise Lost mall to stock up on some various gear, and starts getting to know some of the ex-runners there, a few of whom PPJ and Aradia already know to varying levels.

After resting, they set up the meet through Cat for the glyph job, and drive out to Stapleton to meet someone named “the Guide” on a nature trail in a wildlife reserve. Doing minimal research, they find that the Guide may be the same individual who trained Nicholas Whitebird, GW’s current Voice, as a youth. An older Ute man eventually approaches, after the team realizes that he may have been surveilling them and the nearby area via some wildlife. He introduces himself and seems very friendly and potentially quite powerful.

He explains that he wants the mystery glyphs looked into and ideally demystified, and offers a pretty sizeable payday for it. The team agrees, and he leaves contact number and the vanishes, literally- Aradia and PPJ can barely find any trace, implying he is indeed a skilled mage and high level initiate. They review what they know of the glyphs so far, and decide to run some tests. PPJ buys a burner comm at a service station, and loads the glyph image onto it, while Heat observes to see what happens. Somehow Heat misses the moment, but the glyph vanishes, and Heat himself seems to have been hidden well enough to be unobserved.

They decide to repeat the test on a larger scale, somewhere private, and buy a bunch more burners, and a Faraday bag. They head out to a rural rail bridge, and park underneath, with Creep drugged up to boost his speed with his hand on the Faraday bag’s seal, and Heat keeping an eye in the matrix again. They load the glyph up again and run it on all the devices set to broadcast, and Heat once again misses the first glyph vanishing, but then spots a familiar silhouette icon- it’s the Null Sect!

Heat signals Creep to close it as the Null Clear removes another glyph, and Creep closes the bag, dump-shocking Heat but likely capturing the Null, they hope. After getting the intel from Heat they have a panicked discussion about what to do before Morticia reports two contrails incoming. Otters tries to flee in the van but is unable to dodge the smart missiles, which destroy the vehicle, though the armor holds, protecting the passengers. Morticia then reports two incoming personnel carrier VTOLs en route, though moving slower than the missiles.

Otters send the medical drone off in one direction as a distraction, and the rest of the team scatters, making plans to meet either at the IOND or the safehouse once they ditch any tails. Otters sends Morticia with the Faraday bag to the IOND, with PPJ calling in yet another favor to use a secure Faraday room they have there. They successfully make their exit, with Creep and a few others looking back to see both the van and drone get searched and destroyed. Yikes! Expensive close call. They all stay comms off for a while and sneak back to town via various routes, before many of them meet at the IOND.

They are much relieved to find that Morticia safely arrived and that Black brought her and the bag into the Faraday room without comment, record, or investigation, though he makes clear to PPJ that he best get to repaying some of these favors soon. After some debate about using one of their CEO calls, they decide to instead stick with their Johnson, and call the Guide over to try to get some more money and hand over the bag. The Guide arrives and there is some complex high-level wizard posturing between him and Black, and Black berates PPJ for inviting such a powerful individual inside his wards without his express approval, in a way where the rest of the team can all hear it but are obviously not invited into the conversation.

PPJ returns to the team, humiliated, as TD and Aradia, though only completely implant-free members, strip all tech and head in with the Guide. They brief him, negotiate for more pay, and hand over the bag. He says he will arrange it’s exit quickly from the IOND for further investigation, and chuckles at their brazen nature, saying he had a feeling he’s picked the right team for the job. After all that excitement, the team decides to lay low for a little bit and do some RNR.

Otters heads back to Dracula’s shop in Paradise Lost to start tinkering on the new van he, PPJ, and Creep all split, and Creep tags along to set up a Roseanne clipshow watch at Samantha Hart’s retro tech shop and scout out that part of town a bit. It seems like the ex-runners are training up a local militia and have expanded their safe area for a few blocks around their main base. Aradia, PPJ, and Heat go out clubbing while gets some chill tub time in the safehouse. At the Tower of Babel, Heat eventually finds the basement hacker bar and makes some contacts there.

PPJ, trying to recover from his earlier embarrassment, manages to further embarrass himself but Aradia keeps an eye out and keeps it from getting too bad. Aradia makes contact with a few different info brokers, and all 3 of the team manage to subtly spread around that they are looking for info on the glyphs and will pay for location data or other intel. They head home and rest. The next day as Otters keeps working on the van, Creep has his Roseanne showing, and noone attends except Samantha (whose shop he is renting), and he hides to watch while not being watched. However, Samantha seems interested in his weird remixes and stuff and also his open-mindedness to anti-AI ideas, and invites him to a meeting of some friends of hers.

The 3 partiers gets some calls later in the day from assorted info brokers, and after paying some fees gets leads on 3 glyph locations that are thought to be active now- time to get hustling!! They decide to split into 3 groups and check out the different locations. PPJ and TD head to the Lakeside Amusement Park in Arvada and locate the glyph there, getting a secure offline image before it vanishes shortly after their arrival. It was attached to a critical linchpin on the Ferris Wheel and they couldn’t figure out a better way to analyze it without attracting suspicion.

Creep and Aradia head to the University of Denver in Centennial, and located the Psychology library. Aradia does some social hacking and flirting to get them in, and Creep sneaks by some attendants to the restricted magical area while Aradia distracts. They spot the glyph running on a smart desk, and Heat gets Creep to run a data tap in. This whole library host seems unusually well-protected, though it is a well-funded school and in a tech district, and after conversing briefly with the Sprite doing security, Heat decides not to push it.

However, they do get some useful intel from the desk- someone has carved in “Kara Jay was here!” alongside a fresher carving in a different hand of “RIP”. Aradia inquires with the grad student at the desk and they find out that Kara Jay was a permitted member pre-Crash 2.0 but is not recorded as having any magical talent, just an enthusiasm for arcana, based on her recorded check-outs. Also, some records may have been lost, and she hasn’t been in since 2064.

This glyph also seems older than some of the other ones they’ve seen, and interestingly, hasn’t been removed, yet. They do some research and thinking, and call some info brokers, and are able to piece together that Kara Jay was a magical trinket seller who apparently used this as cover for her true biz as a skilled (but illicit) talismonger. She had a shop in Westminster and ran the shadow side of her biz out of the back, while the front has cheap junk for tourists and wannabes. Also, she flat-lined in Crash 2.0, her daughter kept her alive in a coma til about 10 years ago while trying to keep the shop afloat, before giving up on both, leaving town, and dropping out of contact.

The team decides to surveil the shop a bit and check it out, and head up to the fancy-ish commuter community of Westminster. The shop is on a fairly posh “main street” style shopping arcade, and definitely stands out like a rotten tooth- partially boarded, tons of AR and physical notices of fines pasted up etc, but apparently the building was owned by KJ or someone else who has kept it going for some reason. The store name “the Phoenix” is barely visible in accumulated grime.

After doing their best to look for astral and matrix threats and finding none, Creep sneaks in and pokes around. There are a few plugged in and powered on decent quality commlinks running silent, and a few more fried ones. He grabs one of the fried ones. Nothing else of note stands out- the back room has been cleared out and the only stuff left out front is cheap junk covered in dust and discount stickers. He sneaks back out and the team decides to leave a few people here to surveil while the rest head out to check out the site of the 3rd reported glyph from earlier.

Finding that it is a Triad gambling den, the Motor City survivors on the team call in a favor with Grandmother Biyu to get an in. They also catch up a little on Motor City and the fallout from the Metal Maniacs raid, including what happened with the Chrome Domes in Aurora. They head to the parlor after TD makes contact with their new Triad fixer in town, Bingwen To. He seems to respect Biyu highly, though they are from separate Triads, and suggests that she is powerful enough that a small favor for her is a good opportunity for him.

They head to the parlor and find that the slot machine in question is a smoking ruin, the internal electronics completely fried. They arrange to buy it from To at a discount and bring it back to the safehouse for further investigation, but are unable to glean anything useful from it, aside from that it seems to have been intentionally destroyed in a matrix attack, and that magic was not involved, nor was physical presence. This further suggests Null Sect being behind all this somehow.

Meanwhile, the recon team of Aradia and Heat successfully passes some SIN scans from roving ZDF drones- Westminster seems fairly high security, and also to have a high number of nosey spirit denizens who perhaps reported them as looking suspicious. However, their surveillance pays off as Heat spots some sort of e-life form, badly damaged, arrive into one of the running commlinks. He goes full virtual and makes contact, gradually convincing the form, which takes the appearance of a flaming bird, to allow him to try to help.

After spending some time tweaking software to do what repairs he can, he manages to coax the entity, which refers to itself as “The Seer” but is otherwise incoherent, to migrate to the team’s TacNET-protected RCC, with assistance from StepMAMA, after getting the team’s approval. With no more reported glyph sightings coming in, they decide to do a little more research and then call it in. Aradia pays Kobold to do a bit more digging, and they find out that the assorted bills and taxes on the shop have been kept paid up by one “Dr. Rutherford Kaine”, who after some research they discover is yet another likely false ID of the runner Arcane, who they had previously heard mentioned.

They call The Guide, who instructs them to lay low for a while and provides some fresh working SINs as their ones from the last job may have been burnt from NS involvement. He instructs them to carefully consult Arcane about the glyphs and seek more info, and says he will continue to pay them for this work as well as ongoing exclusivity. PPJ calls him back a little later and gets approval to also share the glyphs with Darien Black to have two expert opinions to compare, but The Guide clarifies that they should share two different overlapping sets of 3/4 of the spotted glyphs with each.

With that taken care of, The Guide tells them to lay low for a few days and lets any NS interested die down before proceeding, and suggests they are welcome to take care of other biz in or out of town in the meantime.

Recon and Retrieval vs GOD
1/26/2024, 12:41:05 PM
Runners
Creep
The Detective
Otters aka Roadkill

After the team’s meeting with Niki the Gardener, they have some recon to tackle. They do some drone surveillance of the provided address, and check out the neighborhood. Seeing no obvious threats, they head over in the van, rotating plates, spoof chips, and electrochromic as they circle the area. The target site is a warehouse that seems to be a bit better defended on the inside than it is made to look on the outside. However, it seems to be mostly operational during the day, and have some decent wards around it, as well as good signal security.

Aradia manages to infiltrate through the ward and makes a deal with the beast spirit that is guarding the site to return with some reagents in exchange for a description of it’s terms of service. She does so, and gets some rough parameters for how to avoid the spirit’s intervention, which it seems willing to interpret in the team’s favor so long as they don’t cross some specific lines, due to the reagent gift. Creep sneaks up and gets a data tap on an exterior camera, and Heat gets control of the camera system and exterior guard comms and loops them.

The team KO the two patrolling guards with a combo of drugs, SNS and magic, and Creep hides their bodies and strips them, while PPJ gets two decent illusion cast on Aradia and TD to look and sound like the guards. They sneak in through the route Creep prepared and put on the guard uniforms, with Aradia using the fashion spell to clean up the uniforms a bit. Creep also found some RFID tokens that seem to maybe also help with passing the ward on the building and hands those over. With Heat still controlling the exterior feeds, he slips them back to “real” seamlessly just as the guard shift change arrives, and Aradia and TD get in with no trouble.

They use their social skills to blend in inside, which is fairly easy, since only a few other guards are present- 1 watches the door controls, while the other 2 patrol the interior, much better armed and armored than those outside. They spot a kit-built shed covered in signal blocking foil, which seems to have a decker inside based on a tiny peek through a crack in the door that TD manages. Aradia slips a data tap into an exterior port of the trailer, seemingly meant for hardline hookups for sensor equipment. The trailer also has a protective ward around it, but it seems to be astral only.

There is also a massive ritual circle that is scarred with weapons and explosives damage, and another medium sized structure, a cube cell filled with slagged electronics. TD manages to sneakily scoop up a sample of these, though they don’t look super recoverable. Meanwhile, Heat starts a hack from outside, spotting the decker’s icon and going for a massive alpha strike with some drug and program assistance. He takes out the decker in one hit, and starts rifling through the well-protected files in the closed system as an audible alarm goes off inside the building.

Aradia and TD, cut off from team comms for opsec, decide that the alarm must mean Heat has done his part, and decide it’s time to get out, seeing guards rushing to secure the doors and racing to the hacker’s shed with a crashcart. They go outside “to secure the perimeter” after not quite nailing their impersonation of knowing what they’re doing with the guns they’re issued, and quickly end up in a small dustup with the 2 guards outside, who luckily are still in their lighter “low profile” armor, and are quickly KO’ed by Creep from hiding.

The team quickly hotfoots it to the van, and they race away before anyone has a chance to follow, Otters doing some skilled pursuit avoidance before they find a safe spot to park nearby and ditch the uniforms and disguises. Driving more chill after that, they arrange a meet with Niki to hand over the goods and do the debrief, and after answering some of her questions, she says they’ll be meeting with the Johnson as well. Heat calls the ZDF on the facility to provide a little further distraction, but fails to spoof his ID, telling them that there is a loose beast spirit who seems to be kept captive there. The team is a little split on this decision.

They head to the meet, at the same krill spot again, after swapping SINs and disguises. They meet Niki and another very femme fatale woman named Vishala, who PPJ and Heat have some trouble playing it cool around. She pays up and tells them that based on their satisfactory performance, she’ll likely have more work for them soon. They head out and rest up at the safehouse, before Heat gets a call from Cat, telling him she needs a team right now for a retrieval job and sending the details of where to meet the Johnson, at a diner not far from their safehouse.

They head out, and meet up with a pro seeming elf woman named Stiletto (possibly referencing her magical dagger, though she seems either non-magical herself or good as masking). Creep makes a point of spotting her bodyguards, but she manages to turn it into her looking even more pro, suggesting they were meant to be noticed and hinting at further unnoticed measures. She gives the team a quick brief to agree to terms before she gets into details, and they accept.

She explains that she was in contact with a security spider who’d fled ARES during their chaotic move, about getting him to somewhere safe and purchasing a data set from him. She has his last location but says the call dropped suddenly after he encountered some sort of trouble on the matrix, and she needs them to go retrieve him and his data, with the data being the higher priority. She passes along his safehouse location in the Warrens, and Otters gets Morticia flying over immediately.

She spots an old scrapyard and auto shop, seemingly with the safehouse on the second floor, defended by turrets, sensors, and mines. However, all the turrets and sensors look deactivated. Otters races the team over in the van while splitting her attention, using assorted fake SINs to enter- as usual it looks easier to go into the barrens of this town than to exit. Otters parks nearby while Creep sneaks up, taking a look at the mines, which also look disabled. He uses a grenade to make surprise trap out of one at the bottom of the stairs to the second floor.

Creep sneaks up the stairs with PPJ joining him, who uses his spectral hand to open the door, who powerful locks have also been disengaged. They see a mess inside- the spider seems to be link-locked, and likely dead or dying, with his smoking deck giving off a disturbing whirring sound. There are various chips and devices scattered around, and a Valkyrie Module straining and failing to stabilize him. He seems to have flopped onto the floor in order to slam a large red button with a bunch of cables running out of it as his last action, which they assume was what disabled all the traps and locks.

Creep tries to heal him, unsuccessfully, before disconnecting the deck, successfully, and then shooting the decker through his headware to make sure not to leave anything recoverable. They head out into the scrapyard and hide as Heat warns of incoming GOD forces- a matrix interdiction just went up on the spotty local grid, and Otters is able to spot a troop carrier VTOL en route. Aradia, thinking fast, casts an illusion of a safe landing zone as the VTOL arrives.

2 GOD soldiers fast rope down to the “safe” area, landing straight on Creep’s explosives trap, doing massive damage to their milspec armor but seemingly not harming the people within. However, this causes the GOD forces to get a lot more serious all of a sudden, and Otters kicks the van into high gear as PPJ and Creep race to board it and they come under fire from a GOD mage and VTOL-mounted miniguns. Heat calls in the Chrome Domes for reinforcements, sending them location data.

The van takes some bad hits, so Otters guns it out of LOS and then hides, rather than fighting, as they see the Chrome Domes coming to their rescue. Heat, fearing a loss of rep, decides to return to help in the fight, but luckily for him is a very slow runners and only gets there to see the aftermath- GOD has wiped out the CD’s leaving a smear of flesh, tech, and moto parts along a few block stretch of the Warrens! Heat sees GOD troopers ripping data from any surviving jacks and confirming their kills, and worries what this may mean for him as he was the one who called them here.

However, for now he must survive, so he stays offline, and sneaks back to the van, where Otters has been doing a quick surface patch with the repair drone to get the stealth coatings working again even though the armor is fucked. They head back to a different exit than they used to enter, using different SINs and a different van spoof. After their SINs pass and they banter with the guards a bit about being lucky to be able to leave after “whatever that was”, they head back to the diner to meet Stiletto.

They pass over the deck in a faraday bag, and she pays up, also telling them that given their solid results she’ll likely have more work soon. With a few jobs under their belts, some new contacts, and a decent payday, they decide to maybe take a few days off and upgrade some ‘ware, rest, patch up the van, and maybe get some fresh ID’s.

Mystery Meat Part 3
1/15/2024, 5:10:22 PM
Runners
Creep
The Detective
Otters aka Roadkill
Pepper Jack
Heat
Aradia

The team redirects their investigation to tracking down the money cow origin after losing their infiltrator drone to the facility’s suspiciously tight security. Otters and Heat do some forum digging on train foamer message boards and figure out where the cattle cars came from that carried the delivery that the money cow was included in. Otters feels confident she can recognize the cow, and they trace the train back to a cattle depot, where a number of ranches bring their herds to sell to bulk buyers. Otters knows it would be normal practice for buyers to have vets on call to examine sample cows from different batches when purchasing, and thinks this could be a good way to get a secret package in.

They buy some fake uniforms and fancy duds, assortedly, as Heat takes them on a little shopping trip around some Hub spots he likes. They also make tentative contact with a pawn shop owner who is rumored to have a sideline in black market goods, though Creep gets the vibe she might not quite be at “missile” level, either in general, or maybe just with them as of yet. They rest up and head to the exchange depot in the morning. Heat uses a TM power to spike one of the old, outdated cameras onsite, and then posing as a repair tech (with uniform + tools) clambers up to access it.

Otters takes the rest of the crew into the vet shed and starts making casual convo for now, with Aradia and TD posing as potential buyers while the rest are security/advisors. Heat hacks the camera and they scrub through the logs from the last few days, and find a strangely feral man leading the cow in question into the vet shed very early the previous morning. Asking around, they ID him as a freelance vet and possibly shapeshifter named RawDog, who only comes in on mondays, which lines up with their speculated schedule.

They call around a bit and do some internet digging to make sure he doesn’t have any obvious problem connections, and finding none, decide to just contact him directly. Aradia manages to get him on the line after about a day of trying, and Heat traces the call to find RD seemingly walking north through the Gap district alongside I-25. Creep hops in the VTOL with the requisite gear and Otters flies him down to intercept, while Aradia cons RD to keep him on the line.

Creep sneaks up behind him after landing, Gamma-Scopolamines him, mage cuffs and hoods him to prevent shifting or spellcasting, and kicks on an area jammer to block comms, with Heat having noticed he has an unusually nice comm for his peripatetic lifestyle. Creep hauls him offroad a little more and hides with him in some scrub brush, seeing some large animals, strange spirits and go-gangs roving the wilds off-highway but remaining unnoticed. The rest of the team shows up about 90 minutes later in the van.

Otters tells RD it’ll all be fine before Aradia and PPJ KO him with spells. Aradia reads his mind, really scraping it clean this time, before they dose him with Laës and Heat scrubs their calls from his comm logs. Otters, feeling a little guilty since this guy seems cool, leaves him a little pile of credsticks too, so he’ll have an interesting time waking up. They find out that he is freelancing for a lawyer, who gives him the small hardcase (filled with data chips and credsticks, though he doesn’t know or ask) once a week to insert into the cow, and then report back with the cows ID tag info to the lawyer and get paid.

They do some research on the lawyer, and Aradia confers with Kobold again, who confirms that his name is on the paperwork for a number of the shell corps stacked up to obscure whoever owns the meat processing plant. They head back to town and stake the lawyer out. He works out of a pretty standard multi-office professional building, which seems to keep regular hours, though his lights remain on after most of the office workers leave.

Aradia projects in and can’t find any life signs so manifests, and narrowly avoids getting spotted by the lawyer, who seems to be working away behind his desk. She dips out and they debate what to do- he’s either fully mechanical, or very magically powerful. They decide to infiltrate. Heat finds a surreptitious camera and hacks it, getting access to building doors and elevators through the system, and PPJ makes himself, Heat, and Creep invisible as Heat gets them up to the lawyer’s floor in the elevator without alerting security.

However, he tells the team they’ll need to move fast, as his Overwatch score is rapidly climbing with all this activity. Creep sneaks into the bathroom ceiling next to the lawyer’s office and makes his way over the wall in the drop ceiling, using his LRAD to scope out the lawyer, who if robotic, is high end to a level the team is not familiar with- autonomic system sounds and everything. They decide to attack from two angles, and PPJ uses his spectral hand to open the door as Creep slides open a ceiling tile. They catch the lawyer by surprise, and Creep hits him with a dart while Heat tags him with some stick’n’shock, shredding his clothes but seemingly mostly bouncing off a non-conductive skin coating he has.

The dart seems to have not effect, harmlessly sticking out of his shoulder. He leaps to his feet, flips the desk up in front of him one-handed (strong!), and his arm splits open as he blasts Heat with a gatling laser. Again- not a tech level the team is familiar with. Heat takes a hard hit but returns fire, as the lawyer also fires on Creep, who drops from the ceiling, badly hurt. Otters jumps into the matrix and starts jamming comms from the office, and the lawyer blasts out and the leaps through a window, surprisingly surviving the 6 story fall and landing in a small crater, which TD sees from across the street.

Aradia runs out of the cafe her and Otters have posted up in a block away, and manages to spot the lawyer and snag him with a levitate spell against his will. Inside, PPJ runs to the window, and hits the lawyer with a lightning bolt, which causes him to dramatically explode into a bunch of mechanical bits. He then spots what looks like a small contrail headed their way. He turns and runs out of the room, yelling “missile”! Creep grabs a rolodex that he spots, and then follows suit, stopping to try to help Heat, who trips on the hallway carpet and eats it.

Creep and Heat both get caught in the blast, with Creep bleeding out, so Heat grabs him as PPJ gets them into another office, and opens the window, so Aradia can levitate them all out, which she does, while also starting to cleanse her astral signature from the area. Otters call a car from her concierge service to a location she spots nearby between cameras, and they all link up there, with the two mages and Otters doing some frantic healing to stabilize Creep and patch Heat up a bit.

They take off in the town car just as ZDF patrol cars arrive on scene, with specialist investigators close on their heels. Otters decides to bribe the driver to be chill, and after some conversation and quick stroll down memory lane (which isn’t entirely welcome for Otters) realizes that Mile High Cab service is likely a retired smuggler’s “made good” business, but that the driver’s are known for their discretion, so long as you don’t bring biz into the cars.

With that resolved, the driver rolls up the partition to leave them to it, and they start looking at the rolodex after Otters rebuffs an attempt by Aradia and TD to inquire into her missing memories. TD manages to piece together some details and names from the rolodex, as well as a bit of a timeline, and after Aradia calls in some assistance from Dean Costello and they do some research, they figure out some general details. It seems like an MCT scientists named Bishek Tam is involved, who was one of the leads on their TM research/torture program (Otters has a disturbing flash memory that she knew he always gave the best price for TM’s but pushes it down).

Interestingly, he went missing shortly before this whole project seems to have begun ramping up, or at least there is no official record of him since then. Also, an ex-NeoNET A-corp called “Matrix Assets INC” seems to be involved, they were bought by Spinrad during the corp shuffle after NN’s dissolution post-Boston CFD revelations, but have since been sold again to an unlisted private buyer, and rumors are their Boston HQ host in now overrun with vines, and is regarded as a death trap for hackers. So this implies the Null Sect either bought or took over MAI, who seem to also be somehow connected to this research program.

Also, the transfer of MAI out from Spin Global’s portfolio apparently occurred shortly before Johnny’s death, and possibly without his knowledge or involvement as he was busy on his acquisition spree and celebratory tour of the world after finally getting his “AAA” rating. So, a larger mystery is afoot, but they finally feel like they have enough info to satisfy the job requirements to the level of pro they seek, and they set up a meet with Cat, Heat using his and Creep’s busted up condition on the call to barter for some extra money.

Cat hosts them in her office at the One-Up Barcade, her spot, after Heat insists on checking that all matrix devices are off and they have taken appropriate counter-surveillance measures. Cat seems to think he’s being overparanoid until he mentions the NS involvement, after which she gets onboard. She seems much less familiar with the NS than the team, but at least broadly aware of their threat level. She thanks them for going the extra mile on the job and pays up, saying she’ll get their name around town as a reliable team.

Heading out with a new fixer contact, Heat sets up a performance in the Aurora Warrens through Jonny Silver, who has a warehouse show planned to celebrate the end of recording the first Glock .50 LP on his Silversounds imprint on Weekday Eclipse, Perianwyr’s new label in exile, named in honor of his destroyed club. The team arrives at the Warrens venue, near the border so freindly-ish to outsiders. They see some gangers doing security and recognize them as Fronts, a large sprawl gang that has been making a lot of the other smaller gangs into vassals of late.

More upsettingly to some on the team, Heat’s biggest fans seem to be a thrill kill cyber gang called the Chrome Domes, who look truly ripe to repeat the problems the team encountered with Metal Maniacs in Detroit. Heat shrugs it off but warns everyone to stay away from the CD’s in the pit during his set. G50 is on first, with Jonny impressarioning from behind a purple rope in the impromptu VIP area. He waves PPJ over but PPJ demurs, for now.

Heat’s set is indecipherably fast to everyone on the team except Otters, who has to go hot to get it, until Creep clips and slows down some segments for everyone else to listen to. The CD’s do indeed go wild in the pit, injuring some unprepared civilians in the process, as Heat sprays them with water from the stage, creating steam cloud off their overclocked bulk cyber-limbs. The crowd goes wild as the cops arrive, and the CD’s start blasting immediately as everyone else scatters. Another successful show!

The team crashes at assorted safehouses, and PPJ gets a weird call from Darien Black in the morning- not only has he not been able to make sense of the glyph yet, it disappeared from his devices while he was researching it. He still has a print out but warns the team to be careful in their research, and says he could maybe learn more with more glyphs, but that he thinks there is a Qabbalistic element, though it may all still be gibberish.

They also get a call from a new fixer, “Niki the Gardener”, who says she is working on behalf of an anonymous Johnson to hire them for another job, having heard good things from Cat. They agree to a meet at a krill restaurant, and go have a fairly pleasant family-style meal with a view of the mountains while talking biz. Niki is very friendly and done up, and tries to flirt with Creep a bit to no avail. This doesn’t seem to dim her charm at all as she gives them the rundown, using some high end counter-surveillance gear and seeming to know enough about it to impress Otters.

They agree to a warehouse break-in and data steal, for kind of low pay, but the job sounds probably not too hard and they feel like it’s another test run of a sort. They do a little research on Niki, and find out that similar to Cat she’s a pretty new fixer who’s getting established- it seems like all the turmoil in Denver lately has lead to some vacancies in the shadow industries. But she is regarded as repaying loyalty with loyalty, and having a background in corp tech or surveillance or something, which explains her gear knowledge and collection.

They prepare to start recon on the site,

Mystery Meat Part 2
1/8/2024, 3:15:38 PM
Runners
Creep
The Detective
Otters aka Roadkill
Pepper Jack
Heat
Aradia

Meanwhile, PPJ had a meeting with Darien Black. They catch up a bit, settling somewhat into their old routine. PPJ gets some emotional satisfaction from Black seeming to almost admit that he would have been sad had he not Returned. Black does insist on testing the color of PPJ’s blood, which implies some level of knowledge on his part as well. PPJ asks Black if he is Black Lodge, and he insists he isn’t, so PPJ shares a lot of info with him, though he holds back about his mystery crystal. PPJ agrees to be studied a bit, but gets a strong feeling he should try to carefully resist giving up certain info, and does so, using his occasionally available bonus power to do so.

Black tells him about what they found, as well as sharing intel about the most recent season of Temple of Terror, and what he thinks has been going on there. However, the IOND examiners seem to have missed the mystery crystal, perhaps mistaking it as part of the blood crystal spine, and Black merely warns PPJ of using the enemies tools, as it is a slippery slope. Black tells PPJ that based on intel they’ve gathered and direct examinations, the IOND thinks that ToT is being used as both some sort of sacrifice ritual, and also to sell or give some of it’s contestants to some entity on the plane of Carcean, which has a bustling trafficking scene.

PPJ seems to have managed to shield his Dis experience, as Black only mentions Carcean and Kadath, but does seem legitimately excited that PPJ was able to Return and seems to be the “real” him, if for no other reason than the expensive education he has received. Black tasks him with doing some investigating into other magical orders and groups in town, looking for Black Lodge infiltration and anything else suspicious, and also suggests he attempt to make contact with the Rogue Lodge. PPJ agrees, gets assigned a tiny dorm room in the IOND hub facility, makes himself at home, and then returns to the team. He feels his magic power grow a bit after he returned to the site of his education and youth.

The team stays up late making a plan for the next day’s shift, hoping to catch some of the mystery employees being smuggled out for the weekend. Creep stays inside the facility overnight avoiding guards. Heat gets PPJ a job there as well by filling in an application for him on the hosts job portal. PPJ, TD, and Aradia each take a separate bus in, and make careful recordings of everyone aboard, trying to memorize all the faces to compare in the evening. Heat and Otters monitor from the van, camo’ed as a rural pest control vehicle. TD also smuggles in a jury-rigged transmitter that Otters built, and gets it to Creep.

While the 3 undercover employees work and keep and eye out for guard patrols and other employees, Creep carefully cuts a hole through the Faraday-caged slaugtherhouse floor, and inserts the transmitter. Otters tests that signal is good but leaves it offline for now. Everyone gets through the day, and Creep stays behind again, planting the snake drone on one of the interior guards before they descend the freight elevator for shift change. Otters and Heat follow the buses in their van, at a safe distance, while Aradia, TD, and PPJ take a look at the other passengers to spot anomolies.

Aradia doesn’t see any on her bus, but TD and PPJ both spot one and assense- TD’s appears mundane, PPJ’s in a technomancer. Heat does a quick head count on surveillance footage of employees leaving, and figures there are 4 extra most likely, so they figure they spotted half of them at least. They decide to converge on the TM, but TD stays on their target just in case, and tails them to a bar, where Aradia heads from her bus dropoff to provide backup if needed.

PPJ tries to tail the TM, who disembarks from the employee bus at a busy transit hub in south Centennial, and is quickly spotted. The TM bolts, and Otters sends another snake drone after him while Heat gives chase on foot, made invisible by PPJ. Otters manages to overclock the drone and tags the TM with a Gamma Scopolamine dart just as he is about to board a train. Heat uses a TM power of his own to jam the train door and get aboard with the now paralyzed TM they were chasing, having PPJ drop the invisibility as people on the platform shout and panic about the snake attack.

Heat tires to play it off as if he is helping the man as the train moves off, while he tries to hack him to get some info. However, the man’s commlink looks to only get turned on every other weekend for 2.5 days, and has no lab info. The train also quickly comes to a stop, with a conductor announcing a “ZDF public safety enforcement action”. A ZDF VTOL quickly comes alongside the raised platform and some troopers pop the door and board the train car as other passengers back away from Heat and the paralyzed man.

A medic comes aboard and takes control of the man, and the other troopers take Heat into custody for questioning, as well as interviewing others on the train quickly and efficiently before exiting and letting the vehicle get back underway. At the station, Heat tries to play it real cool, which mostly works, but his SIN is scanning weirdly (they seem to have high end gear) and since he can’t provide additional ID on the spot, they decide to hold him overnight, at least. Meanwhile, PPJ hops in the van and Otters hightails it out of there, travelling through the Englewood Barrens while switching SINs and van look to ditch pursuit, and getting a little look at that newly dilapidated district in the process- a massive tent city and a KE “Urban” training facility seem like the big destinations, and PPJ gets bad mana vibes, so they exit quickly, passing a SIN scan to do so.

The rest of the team, having lost comms with Heat when he got tossed on the ZDF VTOL, decide to refocus on TD’s target. Aradia manages to chat him up and make plans to go to a second location with her “private car service” while TD watches the exits. Otters and PPJ pull up in the van out front, carefully parking to avoid cameras, and changing the exterior to look like a car service van. Aradia brings the guy out, and they bundle him in, Otters hitting him with GS again and activating a jammer while PPJ yanks him in and Aradia KO’s him with a spell.

Otters changes the van look again a little ways away and carefully drives around Centennial for a while, while Aradia mind probes the guy. She has some trouble getting a good lock on his brain for some reason, but does get some good intel: the lab is studying the intersection/cross effects of Dissonance and Astral phenomena such as rifts. They gets intel on facility layout, number of staff, etc, as well as contact info for the two program leads, one mage and one TM, who each head one side of the facility. However, he has no idea who is paying him and seems just fine with that, as the rate is good and he doesn’t have to do anything explicitly unethical, as he seems purposefully incurious as to where their metahuman test subjects come from.

Deciding to play it safe, and having already seen news coverage and a ZDF press release about the snake drone attack at the transit hub, Aradia calls up her hacker contact Kobold and hires her to erase this guy’s comm, and then she heads solo to the Hub to meet up with Dean Costello and buy a couple doses of Laës. They flirt a bit during the transaction at the Tower of Babel club, which seems to make Dean’s date a bit jealous. Aradia heads back, and Kobold sends along the scraped data and reports job done. They dose the guy with the Laës and drop him out back of a plausible hacker bar in the area, figuring that story will tell itself and he’ll be too embarrassed to report anything.

Aradia also gets a call from her old blackmailed contact Simonetta Chandra, who reports that she will be the team’s point of contact at AZT from now on. She seems to be trying out a little bit of a role reversal powerplay, having seemingly ascended the ranks a bit since Aradia made a clean break with her and gave her some good intel by way of a peaceable parting. Aradia big dogs her back by reporting that the team recently had a convo with AZT’s CEO, and Chandra blanches a bit but mostly brazens through it, saying that she’ll be in touch when AZT has need of their services next, and will of course be happy to help supply the team for their criminal activities in GW’s domain as needed.

Aradia hires Kobold again to do some digging into the two program leads as the lab, as well as any ownership data on the facility, while the team rests. Otters makes one quick connection to the snake drone inside the facility, sneaking it to a good hiding spot, and then shutting down again til the next day. They rest, and get some intel when they awaken- the whole property is owned by a series of shell corps registered in various offshore domains, so not much to go on there. And not a ton on the program leads either- the TM is ex-NeoNET, and regarded as somewhat of a traitor to her kind, and the mage is a sort of adjunct for hire who has bounced around between various academic and corp positions without sticking anywhere longterm, but with a mid-quality specialization in astral phenomena practical research.

Both have a rep for being more interested in money than morals, and both went dark about 6 months back, seeming to live at the facility fulltime. This checks out with what the team has seen from the other employees there as well. They also find out the TM is a big fan of Aztlaner cutlure and history, but has no known AZT ties. They drive back out to Castle Rock, conceal themselves as pest control again, and Otters starts sneaking around with the drone. She gets a good look at half the facility, luckily emerging into the magic half, which presumably has lower chance of being detected.

She gets some more experimental data, scans the personnel, and sees that the two halves of the facility are thoroughly kept apart- they talk via old school hardwired vid monitors, and have no wireless connections from side to side. There seem to be up to 4 “subjects” detained on each side, and 8 staff during the week, with rotating weekend breaks. The 9 security guards rotate between the hardened safe room Creep saw, and 4 upstairs/2 on call to each side patrols, while resting. The staff all seem fairly friendly and comfortable with each other. Otters is also able to observe another video meeting between the two program heads, and gets some research data off a screen.

She also overhears them talking about pay and next instructions arriving monday, so decides to keep the drone around to try to get more intel. She sneaks it into the plumbing to hide from what looks like a weekly sunday bug sweep, and puts it in standby for now. The undercover employees head back to work monday, and keep an eye out for anything suspicious, eventually spotting the manager separating one of the cows from the herd to work on himself, and then handing off a sealed case to one of the interior security guards.

They leave, and Otters sneaks the snake back out of the plumbing and hides in the main guard break room, waiting for one of them to bring the case in, which they do before long. Cracking it open, Otters can see two data chips, as well as what looks like one certified credstick per underground employee. The guards leave 4 behind and split the rest into two piles, each taking one out into one wing. Otters follows into the magic wing again, and see the guard happily distributing pay to the various lab techs.

Otters follows the guard to the program heads’ office and sneaks in, where the guard gives him one of the data chips and the final credstick before exiting. The mage goes over to the wall and hits a big safety switch, and Otters looses connection to the drone. The team sticks around for a bit, til some of the interior guards come out equipped with scoped long guns and start scanning the horizon. They take off and head back to town, with the intent of looking into cattle stuff next, to see where the packages are getting inserted.

Otters also sets up a crawler drone with a commlink to run her new high end clean SIN in her fancy but unappointed Hub apartment, and sets an agent to do some occasional matrix activity on it while it moves around the apartment throughout the day, just to establish a viable cover, as she got spooked by how hard it is to run in a town with cops and SIN scanners again after getting accustomed to Detroit. The Detective takes a lengthy break to relax in an opiate haze in a tub for a while.
